a:Taa1,方法核心是 Consistency Forcing（CForce）。训练起点是一个预训练好的 dLLM。作者先用该模型在自己的解码策略下生成 self-rollout trajectories，而不是使用 teacher rollout 或 oracle trajectory。这样做的原因是，学生在推理时真正会遇到的状态分布，来自它自己在 threshold decoding 下逐步 reveal token 的轨迹；如果训练时看的是别的轨迹，尤其是信息条件更优的 teacher 轨迹，蒸馏目标就会偏离真实推理状态。

接着，作者不按 native denoising step 逐步对齐，而是把轨迹按“每揭示固定数量的新 token”切成若干 stage。这样做有两个考虑：一是存储 native step 全轨迹成本高；二是 reveal-count 更直接对应高并行解码下的承诺强度。对于每一对相邻 stage，训练目标是让早期 stage 在 still-masked positions 上的预测分布，去匹配同一模型在后期 stage 的 stop-gradient 预测。这里没有冻结 teacher，teacher 就是同一个模型在更后状态下的输出。直观上，后期状态拥有更多上下文，尤其 edit-capable 模型还可能经过 T2T refinement，因此它对某些位置的判断更可信，可以反过来监督早期 M2T 预测。

关键损失设计有两部分。第一部分是 Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence（CAD），用于 still-masked positions 的分布对齐。作者没有固定用 forward KL 或 reverse KL，而是根据 later-stage prediction confidence 动态插值。原因在于两者偏好不同：forward KL 更覆盖 teacher 支持集，reverse KL 更偏 mode-seeking。后期预测若置信度高，更适合强调 mode-seeking；若置信度低，则需要更保守地保留分布质量。CAD 试图把这两种偏好按置信度自适应结合。第二部分是 cross-entropy anchor，用于 reveal-boundary token，也就是那些在当前阶段即将被提交或刚被揭示的 token。这个 CE 项的作用是稳定 token commitment，防止纯分布对齐把模型推向“看起来和后期一致，但不敢明确提交”的状态。

此外，作者加入了 reveal difficulty curriculum。因为越早期、越高并行的阶段越难，直接在最激进的 reveal 设置上做相邻阶段蒸馏容易不稳定。课程学习让模型先在较容易的 reveal 条件下学会一致性，再逐步过渡到更难的高并行情形。理论部分则给出一个上界分析：早期阶段预测误差可由相邻阶段分布漂移和 reveal-boundary token error 两项控制，这正好对应 CAD 和 CE anchor。这个理论不是完整收敛证明，但它把损失设计和误差来源一一对应起来，解释了为什么不是只用 KL，也不是只用 CE。b:T436,This is a meaningful contribution to alternative language modeling paradigms. First, it reframes dLLM efficiency around early-stage reliability rather than step count alone, which is closer to the actual failure mode in high-TPF decoding. Second, it improves training-inference alignment by distilling along the model’s own self-rollout threshold-decoding trajectories instead of relying on external teachers or privileged trajectories. That design choice is particularly important for diffusion LMs, where the state distribution seen at inference can differ sharply from teacher-generated trajectories. Third, it adapts consistency-style training to discrete masked diffusion language models with a confidence-adaptive KL objective and a token-commitment anchor, both tailored to the actual sources of decoding error. Fourth, the empirical pattern is informative: edit-capable models can gain both speed and quality, while non-edit models show a clearer speed-quality trade-off. That distinction says something substantive about where the extra supervision signal comes from.c:T9e3,The method, Consistency Forcing (CForce), starts from a pretrained dLLM and collects self-rollout trajectories under the model’s own decoding policy. This is a deliberate choice. The states that matter are the ones the student will actually encounter under threshold decoding, not cleaner or more informative states produced by a separate teacher. Using self-rollouts therefore reduces the mismatch between training supervision and inference-time state distribution.

The collected trajectory is then partitioned into stages, not at every native denoising step, but after a fixed number of newly revealed tokens. This stage definition is important for two reasons: it lowers storage overhead, and it ties supervision directly to reveal intensity, which is what controls practical parallelism. For each adjacent stage pair, the model is trained so that the earlier stage matches a stop-gradient prediction from the same model at the later stage on positions that remain masked. There is no frozen teacher. The later stage acts as a stronger target because it has more observed context, and in edit-capable models it may also incorporate token-to-token refinements that correct earlier drafts.

The loss has two main components. The first is Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence (CAD), used to align distributions on still-masked positions. Instead of choosing either forward KL or reverse KL globally, CAD interpolates between them based on the confidence of the later-stage prediction. The intuition is straightforward: high-confidence later predictions justify a more mode-seeking target, while lower-confidence predictions call for a more conservative distributional match. The second component is a cross-entropy anchor on reveal-boundary tokens, which stabilizes token commitment. Without this anchor, pure distribution matching could improve consistency while weakening the model’s ability to make decisive commitments at the reveal boundary.

The paper also introduces a curriculum over reveal difficulty. Early-stage, high-parallelism supervision is the hardest regime, so training directly on the most aggressive reveal settings can be unstable. The curriculum gradually increases difficulty. The theoretical section supports the design by showing that early-stage prediction error can be bounded by adjacent-stage distribution drift and reveal-boundary token error, which map directly onto the CAD and CE terms. It is not a full convergence theory, but it does provide a clean justification for why both terms are needed.d:T78b,The headline results are on two LLaDA variants. On the edit-capable LLaDA2.1-mini, CForce increases average Tokens Per Forward from 6.94 to 9.08 while also improving average accuracy from 85.57 to 86.41. That is the strongest result in the paper because it shows simultaneous gains in speed and quality, consistent with the claim that later T2T-refined states provide useful supervision for earlier M2T predictions. On the non-edit LLaDA2.0-mini, CForce raises average TPF from 3.60 to 6.42, but the effect is better described as an explicit speed-quality trade-off. The model performs better under aggressive few-step decoding and at fixed TPF budgets, yet the gains are not as cleanly dual-improving as in the edit-capable case.

The full paper also appears to include several important analyses. One is performance under different decoding budgets or threshold aggressiveness, which should show that the gains concentrate in the high-parallelism regime rather than uniformly across all settings. Another is loss ablation: CAD versus plain forward KL or reverse KL, with and without the CE anchor. That ablation is important because the method’s credibility depends on showing that confidence-adaptive interpolation is not just cosmetic. A third likely analysis is the effect of the reveal-difficulty curriculum on stability and final few-step accuracy. Finally, the comparison between edit-capable and non-edit models is conceptually important. It helps separate gains from simply having more context later in the trajectory versus gains from having genuinely improved later states due to token-level refinement.

When reading the experiments closely, pay attention to how accuracy is defined, how TPF is measured, what block-diffusion setup is used, and what the storage and compute overhead of self-rollout trajectory collection looks like. Those details determine whether the method is practical beyond the paper’s scale.e:T5d4,Start with the introduction and preliminaries. You need a clear picture of block diffusion, mask-to-token decoding, token-to-token refinement, and threshold decoding before the method will make sense. In particular, the distinction between edit-capable and non-edit dLLMs is central to understanding why the gains differ across models.

Then read the method section carefully, focusing on self-rollout trajectory construction, stage partitioning, CAD, and the CE anchor. Three questions are worth keeping in mind: why self-rollouts are preferable to teacher trajectories, why stages are defined by reveal count rather than native denoising steps, and why KL direction should depend on later-stage confidence. The theory section is worth reading alongside the method because it ties the loss design to a concrete error decomposition.

In the experiments, first inspect the main figures and tables showing the LLaDA2.1-mini speed-and-quality improvement and the LLaDA2.0-mini trade-off. Then move to the ablations, especially CAD versus forward/reverse KL, the effect of the CE anchor, and the curriculum. The deepest idea in the paper is not a single metric gain. It is the training-inference alignment principle: train early states to learn from the model’s own later, more informative states along the actual decoding trajectory it will follow at inference. A quick read takes about 40 minutes. A serious read for reproduction or adaptation to another dLLM will likely take 1.5 to 2 hours.f:T429,QUASAR separates reconstruction into quantization and dequantization. Quantization maps full-precision weights to discrete codes while treating the clipping range as a searchable variable. Dequantization maps those codes back to reconstructed weights using learned scales, and optionally offsets for asymmetric quantization. The reconstruction objective is not plain L2 error; it is a Hessian-weighted, loss-aware reconstruction error inspired by second-order PTQ. To make this usable inside QAT, QUASAR approximates the Hessian diagonal with an exponential moving average of squared gradients, giving per-parameter saliency scores. For a small set of candidate clipping ranges, it fixes the discrete code assignment, solves the dequantization parameters in closed form by saliency-weighted least squares, and chooses the candidate with the smallest loss-aware reconstruction error. The analysis decomposes the QAT convergence bound into initialization, minibatch noise, and reconstruction error; only the last term is directly controlled by the reconstruction map.10:T427,FreeBalance performs pre-routing online balancing through residual workload prediction. The key observation is that the input to an MoE router can be approximated well enough from the residual representation produced by the preceding layer. So, before the current router executes, the system feeds that residual into the corresponding router to obtain a lightweight prediction of the upcoming expert workload. This prediction is used only for scheduling, not for model execution. Based on the predicted load, FreeBalance adjusts expert-device mapping and starts expert-weight migration immediately, overlapping migration with pre-routing computation stages, especially attention. To avoid over-migration, the method includes a cost model that limits how many experts can be moved given the available overlap window. In other words, it does constrained balancing rather than chasing the most even load at any cost. Actual MoE execution still uses the original router outputs, so the model’s outputs remain unchanged; the gain comes purely from better scheduling.11:T596,The method has three main parts. First, compensation is moved from block level to individual linear layers. Instead of sharing one compensator across a Transformer block, the paper attaches separate compensators to each of the four linear layers. This matters because quantization error structure differs substantially across layers. The fitting is also sequential rather than one-shot: after compensating one layer, the compensated layer is inserted back into the model, and the next layer is solved using the true downstream input distribution under upstream quantization and compensation. This avoids train-deploy mismatch. Second, the core contribution is a numerically stable solver based on a truncated pseudoinverse. Rather than adding damping or ridge regularization to all directions, the method removes only the collapsed singular directions and preserves the remaining compensable subspace. That is the conceptual difference from prior stabilizers: they shrink everything; QuaSAR tries to delete only the directions that make the solution explode. Third, the compensation itself is compressed for deployment through low-rank factorization and compensation quantization, giving a practical accuracy-size tradeoff. The paper also studies budget allocation and argues that global rank budgeting is more meaningful than fine-grained per-layer allocation, because layer importance does not reliably predict rank sensitivity.12:T506,Several results are especially informative. On ViT-B under W4A4, the paper highlights layer b0.fc2 as a canonical failure case: prior gating reports R² = -68.6 and a compensation norm of 6.2×10^7, so the layer is confidently discarded. Yet it is actually the single most valuable compensator in the model; removing it costs 1.05% top-1 accuracy, more than any other layer. This directly validates the paper’s claim that negative R² can be a sign of numerical collapse rather than low utility. In the main result, the method reaches 81.42% top-1 on ViT-B/W4A4 without additional training, outperforming prior training-free methods and, according to the paper, even a fine-tuning-based QwT variant. With low-rank decomposition and compensation quantization, it obtains a more deployable point at 80.26% top-1 and 54.7 MB, filling the gap between the heavier but stronger QwT and the lighter but weaker QwT-v2. The layer-wise design gives a +3.1% gain over block-wise compensation. Another notable ablation is rank allocation: reversing a “high-rank to important layers” policy changes accuracy by only 0.02%, suggesting that layer importance and rank sensitivity are largely decoupled. The paper also reports that the same failure mechanism appears in both CNNs and Transformers.13:T46a,Start with the introduction example around negative R² and the wrongly discarded layer; it gives the clearest intuition for why the paper exists. Then read the method section with one question in mind: why is rank deficiency, not generic ill-conditioning, the decisive failure mode? After that, focus on three pieces. First, the sequential layer-wise fitting procedure and why downstream inputs must be recollected after each compensation step. Second, the truncated pseudoinverse construction and how it differs from ridge or damped inverses. Third, the budget-allocation study, which is easy to overlook but carries a useful systems-level message for deployable compensation. In the experiments, prioritize the main ViT-B/W4A4 table, the single-layer removal analysis, the block-wise vs layer-wise ablation, and the accuracy-size tradeoff after compression. If reading more deeply, two follow-up questions are worth testing mentally: whether the truncation rule is stable across architectures, and whether the same diagnosis can improve LLM PTQ methods such as GPTQ, QERA, or CALDERA in weight-only or mixed-precision settings.14:T401,The paper provides a clean localization result with direct implications for reproducibility, benchmarking, and serving: nominally equivalent quantized linear kernels do not guarantee bitwise-equivalent end-to-end outputs, and the divergence does not come from the INT32 accumulator, which under a verified no-overflow bound is exact and order-independent, but from the small post-accumulation stage of scale application and output rounding. Its value to pretrain and systems research is twofold. First, it shows that strict numerical comparisons across kernels, backends, or hardware must control these boundary-precision differences, because ulp-level perturbations can be amplified by autoregressive decoding into completely different token sequences. Second, it offers a differential-testing recipe that transfers to other low-precision inference formats such as FP8 and microscaling, useful for diagnosing hidden inconsistencies among post-training quantization pipelines, inference engines, and hardware implementations.15:T892,方法上，作者把生成过程写成两阶段：先由 MLLM 根据文本提示生成中间表示 D，再由 DiT 在文本和 D 的条件下生成视频。论文的核心不是提出一个单点模块，而是系统比较三类设计。

第一，中间表示 D 的形式。作者重点考察显式 visual-semantic tokens，而不是自然语言重写或隐式 latent query。显式 token 又分连续特征和离散语义 token。离散化部分比较了 EMA-based tokenizer 与 embedding-based tokenizer，并考察是否加入额外监督，如 MLLM understanding loss 或 pixel reconstruction loss。结论是 EMA 码本更稳，因为它保留原始特征尺度，不依赖梯度更新 codebook，也不需要额外辅助目标；而层次化 token 结构虽然能编码从浅层感知到深层语义的多级信息，但在视频生成里带来的收益有限。

第二，MLLM 如何生成这些 token。作者比较 causal attention 与 full attention。full attention 允许所有视觉 token 双向交互，理论上更利于整体表示；但实验显示 causal attention 更适合语义 token 的时序生成，因为它与 next-token 预测一致，也更贴合视频事件按时间展开的结构。这里的关键判断不是“因果注意力更强”，而是中间层 token 的任务是规划时序语义，而不是重建静态视觉细节，因此生成式约束反而更合适。

第三，DiT 如何融合 MLLM 输出。作者比较两条路线：一条是 query/connector 式 latent bridging，即把 MLLM 输出压成隐式条件；另一条是显式 visual token conditioning，把生成出的语义 token 直接注入 DiT。显式条件又比较 self-attention 与 cross-attention 注入方式。结论是多层 cross-attention 最有效，因为它让语义 token 在去噪全过程持续提供结构约束，而不是只在某个浅层阶段提供弱提示。

基于这些结论，作者提出 BiVidGen：使用 EMA tokenizer 产生离散语义 token，MLLM 以 causal attention 自回归生成这些 token，DiT 通过显式多层 cross-attention 消费这些 token 进行扩散渲染。这个设计的重点是把 MLLM 从“编码器”变成“规划器”。16:T8f4,The generation process is factorized into two stages: an MLLM first produces an intermediate representation D from the text prompt, and a DiT then generates the video conditioned on both the prompt and D. The contribution is not a single module, but a systematic comparison along three design axes.

First, the form of D. The paper focuses on explicit visual-semantic tokens rather than prompt rewriting in natural language or implicit latent queries. Within explicit tokens, it compares continuous visual features against discrete semantic tokens. For discretization, the authors study EMA-based tokenizers and embedding-based tokenizers, with and without auxiliary objectives such as MLLM understanding loss or pixel reconstruction loss. Their conclusion is that EMA-based tokenization is the most stable choice because it preserves feature scale, avoids gradient-updated codebooks, and does not require extra supervision. They also test hierarchical token structures inspired by recent VLM designs, but find limited gains for video generation.

Second, how the MLLM should generate those tokens. The paper compares causal attention and full attention over visual tokens. Full attention allows bidirectional interaction and may help holistic representation learning, but causal attention works better in practice for semantic-token generation. The reason is structural: these tokens are used for temporal planning, and autoregressive generation matches the sequential nature of video events.

Third, how the DiT should use the MLLM output. The authors compare latent bridging through learnable queries and connector modules against explicit visual-token conditioning. For explicit conditioning, they test different injection mechanisms, including self-attention and cross-attention. Multi-layer cross-attention performs best, suggesting that semantic tokens are most useful when they provide persistent structural guidance throughout denoising rather than a weak one-shot hint.

These findings are combined into BiVidGen: an EMA-based tokenizer produces discrete semantic tokens, the MLLM generates them autoregressively with causal attention, and the DiT consumes them through explicit multi-layer cross-attention. The key shift is to use the MLLM as a planner rather than just an encoder.17:T5d2,The main strength of the experiments is the systematic ablation, not a single headline number. On VBench-Long, BiVidGen outperforms the compared design variants, and the qualitative examples show better temporal consistency, more coherent causal transitions, and stronger semantic alignment.

The main empirical findings are:
1. Explicit visual tokens work better than natural-language expansion or implicit latent bridging, likely because they provide a more direct and structured conditioning signal to the DiT.
2. EMA-based tokenization is more stable and more effective than embedding-based codebooks and variants with extra supervision.
3. Hierarchical token structures bring limited benefit, suggesting that the current bottleneck is high-level semantic planning and temporal organization rather than packing more multi-level visual detail into the interface.
4. Causal attention outperforms full attention for semantic-token generation, producing more structured temporal plans and better downstream video quality.
5. Explicit token conditioning is better than query-based latent connectors, and within explicit conditioning, multi-layer cross-attention is the strongest fusion strategy.

The provided full-text excerpt does not include a complete table of model sizes, data scale, or all exact metric values, so it would be unsafe to invent precise numbers. The right way to read the results is to focus on the ablations along the three design axes rather than only the final score.18:T55a,Read it as an interface-design paper, not as a standard video-generation paper.

Start with the three questions in the introduction: what to pass, how to generate it, and how to fuse it. That is the real structure of the paper.

Then go to the method section and focus on the definition of the intermediate representation D, followed by the tokenizer, attention, and fusion choices. The useful lens is that the paper is defining an MLLM-to-DiT protocol. Two questions are worth keeping in mind while reading: why discrete tokens are preferable to continuous features here, and why causal token generation helps more than full attention in video.

In the experiments, prioritize three ablation groups:
1. intermediate representation choices;
2. causal vs full attention;
3. latent bridging vs explicit token conditioning, especially the number and placement of cross-attention layers.

If time is limited, the main tables and ablation figures plus the corresponding method subsections are enough to capture the core message in about 30–45 minutes. For deeper reading, two follow-up questions are worth exploring: whether these discrete semantic tokens could become a shared interface across image, video, and audio generation, and whether the planning-rendering split can be jointly optimized end to end during pretraining rather than assembled from adapted components.19:T9c3,方法上分成三块。第一块是环境与训练系统。作者构建了一个 agent-agnostic 的交互框架，把环境模拟、对话执行、模型推理和训练器分离。这样做的原因很直接：多回合社会交互训练不是单轮 preference optimization，训练信号来自完整 episode 的效用、协议是否达成、对手策略等，若系统耦合过紧，很难支持跨环境训练、黑盒 teacher、或不同对手组合。六个环境覆盖了多物品分配、带私有偏好的资源分配、价格谈判、工作面试式协商、日程协调和市场匹配，交互结构差异较大，便于观察迁移是否来自表面模板还是更抽象的策略能力。

第二块是 SocialRL 本身。论文先在每个环境里训练 specialist policy，再做完整 cross-environment transfer matrix。虽然给出的摘要没有展开具体 RL 算法细节，但从全文描述看，核心不是发明一个全新的 policy gradient 变体，而是把社会交互中的效用函数、对手建模、episode rollout 和后续 consolidation 组织成一套可复用 recipe。训练目标围绕 principal utility，而不是单纯的 helpfulness 或 agreement rate。也就是说，模型被奖励去争取更优结果、保护私有信息、在不利条件下拒绝成交，而不是“把对话聊顺”。这和常规 assistant RLHF/RLAIF 的目标函数有根本差异。

第三块是 specialist→generalist 的整合，以及显式 ToM 蒸馏。作者用了两条 consolidation 路线：cascade RL，以及 multi-teacher mode-seeking OPD（MOPD）。前者更像课程式地把多个环境能力串联进一个统一策略；后者则利用多教师策略分布，把统一模型往高价值模式上拉，而不是简单平均教师行为。这里“mode-seeking”很关键，因为谈判策略往往多峰，直接行为克隆容易学成折中而无效的平均策略。除此之外，论文引入 Infer→Act→Anticipate 轨迹，把模型在某一状态下对对手偏好/意图的推断、当前动作选择、以及对对手下一步反应的预期串成监督信号，再蒸馏进策略模型。这样做不是为了让模型生成更长的解释，而是为了把 social reasoning 拆成可训练的中间变量。全文的分析显示，三者里对 performance 最有预测力的是 Anticipate，尤其是 next-action prediction，这说明有效策略更依赖“对手接下来会怎么动”的局部博弈预测，而不是抽象人格归因。1a:T434,Its importance for foundation-model research is threefold. First, it treats social reasoning as a trainable post-training capability rather than a byproduct of frontier scale. The central empirical claim is that targeted RL and distillation can push a 4B model close to, and in some settings beyond, much larger GPT-class models on strategic interaction tasks. That is a meaningful data point in the scale-vs-signal debate. Second, it contributes a reusable infrastructure for multi-turn social interaction training: environment, agent harness, inference engine, and trainer are decoupled, which matters for RL over long dialogues, heterogeneous opponents, and black-box teachers. Third, it studies explicit theory-of-mind supervision in a way that is operational rather than decorative. Infer→Act→Anticipate traces are distilled into the policy, and the analysis shows that anticipating the counterpart’s next action is the ToM component most predictive of negotiation utility. That is a useful lesson for anyone designing intermediate supervision for agentic behavior.1b:T958,The method has three layers. The first is the training system. The authors build an agent-agnostic interaction stack where the environment simulator, dialogue execution harness, inference backend, and trainer are separated. This is not just an engineering convenience. In multi-turn strategic interaction, the learning signal depends on full episodes, opponent behavior, and utility outcomes. A tightly coupled setup makes it hard to support cross-environment training, heterogeneous opponents, or distillation from black-box policies. The six environments span allocation, multi-issue bargaining, price negotiation, interview-style negotiation, calendar coordination, and marketplace interaction, giving enough structural diversity to test whether the model learns reusable strategic abstractions.

The second layer is SocialRL itself. The paper first trains domain specialists, then evaluates the full transfer matrix across environments. The contribution is less about inventing a new low-level RL optimizer and more about defining a complete recipe for strategic delegation: principal-utility-centered rewards, multi-turn rollouts, opponent-conditioned interaction, and evaluation under held-out scenarios. The objective is explicitly different from standard assistant alignment. The model is rewarded for protecting private information, holding reservation boundaries, and rejecting bad deals when necessary, rather than maximizing agreement rate or perceived helpfulness.

The third layer is specialist-to-generalist consolidation plus explicit ToM distillation. The authors use cascade RL and multi-teacher mode-seeking OPD (MOPD) to merge specialists into a unified policy. The mode-seeking aspect matters because negotiation policies are often multi-modal; naive averaging or plain behavior cloning can collapse distinct but effective strategies into a weak compromise. On top of that, the paper introduces Infer→Act→Anticipate traces as structured supervision. These traces encode the model’s inference about the counterpart, the chosen action, and the expected next move from the counterpart. The point is not to make the model verbose, but to expose trainable intermediate variables for social reasoning. The analysis suggests that among these components, next-action anticipation is the strongest predictor of downstream utility, which is a concrete and actionable finding.1c:T74f,The experimental story has four main parts. First, domain specialists are trained and evaluated on held-out scenarios in each of the six environments. The headline result is that 4B specialist policies achieve utility comparable to much larger GPT models across the suite, showing that targeted post-training can compensate for a large part of the scale gap in this setting. Second, the paper reports a full cross-environment transfer matrix. Transfer is substantial but not uniform: it follows interaction structure. Environments with similar bargaining dynamics transfer better to each other than environments that only share surface language. That is an important result because it suggests the model is learning reusable strategic patterns rather than memorizing domain-specific scripts.

Third, the specialists are consolidated into a single 4B policy. Using cascade RL and MOPD, the unified model reaches an average utility of 0.627 across the six environments, compared with 0.625 for GPT-4.1, 0.619 for GPT-5.1, and 0.613 for GPT-5.2 in the paper’s evaluation. The right interpretation is not that the small model is broadly stronger, but that objective-task alignment can outweigh generic assistant priors on strategic delegation benchmarks.

Fourth, the paper studies explicit theory-of-mind supervision. Distilling Infer→Act→Anticipate traces improves both performance and cross-environment generalization. The most informative subskill is next-action prediction: it is the ToM signal most correlated with negotiation success. While the excerpt does not expose every ablation number, the full paper appears to include comparisons with and without ToM traces, differences between consolidation methods, and analyses of specialist versus unified policies. The transfer matrix and ToM correlation analyses are likely the most informative tables and figures.1d:T596,Start with the problem framing. The key idea is that assistant alignment and strategic delegation optimize different behaviors. If that point is not clear, the rest of the paper can look like a niche negotiation benchmark. Then read the environment and infrastructure sections carefully. For readers interested in multi-turn RL, agent training systems, or post-training for delegated action, this part is as valuable as the policy-learning recipe.

Next, focus on the specialist training results and the full transfer matrix. Those sections answer whether SocialRL is learning domain templates or more abstract interaction structure. After that, read the consolidation section in detail, especially the difference between cascade RL and MOPD. If your work touches continual post-training, multi-task RL, or teacher aggregation, this is where the paper becomes most reusable.

Finish with the Infer→Act→Anticipate section and its ablations. The important question is not whether the model can produce reasoning traces, but which intermediate supervision actually improves utility. Pay special attention to the analysis showing that next-action prediction is the most predictive ToM skill. For a 45-minute pass, read the task setup, the six-environment suite, the transfer matrix, the unified-policy main table, and the ToM ablations. For a deeper read, spend extra time on the training infrastructure and consolidation details.1e:T8c0,The technical core is the experimental design. Training data comes from Multilingual Reasoning Gym, which procedurally generates reasoning problems in 14 languages and supports adjustable difficulty. Instead of simply translating English datasets, the authors use an online curriculum: for each task, difficulty increases when more than 70% of the last 32 rollouts are correct and decreases when fewer than 10% are correct. This matters because the same model can have very different capability baselines across languages, so fixed difficulty would confound language effects with reward availability. They also pre-filter tasks using Qwen3-14B-Base at easy difficulty, removing tasks with pass@8 = 0, and exclude heavily English-dependent tasks from training while keeping them for evaluation. The resulting setup uses 62 training tasks and 30 held-out tasks for OOD evaluation.

Training uses a DAPO-style GRPO objective without KL penalty. Each run uses 500 steps, learning rate 1e-6, 64 prompts per step, and 8 rollouts per prompt. Rewards consist of a binary correctness reward plus two auxiliary rewards, each weighted at half of correctness: a format reward and a reasoning-language reward. The reasoning-language reward is instantiated either as English reasoning or native-language reasoning. A key implementation detail is that auxiliary rewards are only granted when the final answer is correct, which prevents the model from gaming language or format rewards at the expense of solving the problem. The paper notes that preliminary runs in low-resource languages could otherwise collapse or overfit to formatting.

The model suite spans Qwen, Gemma, and SmolLM families, including both base and post-trained variants from 1B to 8B parameters. The authors run monolingual GRPO for 11 MGSM languages and one multilingual run with uniform language sampling. Italian, Korean, and Portuguese are fully held out as unseen languages. For model-language pairs with sufficient initial support for the target language, they additionally train native-reward variants. The point of this design is to disentangle training language, rewarded reasoning language, and model pretraining background rather than to optimize a single benchmark score.1f:T6a8,The most robust findings are fourfold. First, native-language reasoning rewards usually trail English-reasoning rewards by only a small margin on average; there is no universal large penalty for asking the model to reason in the prompt language. Second, cross-lingual transfer is strong: GRPO in one language often improves performance in many others, suggesting that RL is transferring problem-solving behavior rather than only surface-form language patterns. Third, the transfer is not monotonic. The paper identifies a regression failure mode where training in a particular language improves some evaluations but causes substantial drops on unseen languages or tasks. These regressions are highly model- and language-specific. Fourth, multilingual training is not always better than the best monolingual run, but it often provides a more stable compromise and is especially relevant for held-out-language generalization.

Evaluation covers seen tasks, 30 unseen tasks, MGSM, and PolyMath. The abstract does not expose the full numeric tables, but the full paper makes clear that the conclusions come from a broad comparison over 9 models, 11 training languages, monolingual versus multilingual runs, and English versus native reasoning rewards. For a pretraining researcher, the more useful part is the analysis style: the authors do not stop at average scores, but inspect transfer matrices across languages and compare how different model families react to the same training language. That supports a practical conclusion: the main bottleneck in multilingual RL is not merely data availability, but the interaction among initial language competence, reward attainability, and policy drift during RL.20:Ta81,The method has three main components. First, alignment is performed in shared text space rather than token-id space. The student generates a response under its own tokenizer and chat template, then decodes it into surface text. The teacher is not fed student tokens directly; instead, the teacher reconstructs its own context using its own chat template, appends the exact same student response text, and re-tokenizes the full sequence with its native tokenizer. Distillation is then applied only at positions where a student token and a teacher token correspond to exactly the same text span. This avoids inventing artificial token correspondences across incompatible vocabularies and also sidesteps differences in chat formatting. The trade-off is that not every token can be aligned, but the paper argues that sparse high-confidence alignment is preferable to dense but noisy alignment.

Second, the paper addresses the central failure mode in long-teacher to short-student OPD: uncontrolled response-length growth. In naive OPD, the teacher evaluates each student-generated step. If the teacher itself prefers much longer chains of thought, the student is repeatedly encouraged to continue generating, which causes the thinking segment and final answer to grow until truncation becomes common. To counter this, the authors add a student reference KL loss that keeps the current student policy close to its initial policy. The role of this term is not generic regularization; it specifically limits excessive policy drift toward a trajectory distribution the student cannot support within its context budget. The paper explicitly notes that larger teacher-student gaps typically require stronger KL regularization.

Third, the authors mask advantages on special termination tokens. Their observation is that direct teacher supervision often discourages the student from emitting tokens such as </think> or <|im_end|>, because the teacher would prefer to continue reasoning. If this signal is passed through unchanged, the student learns to delay termination and training quickly becomes pathological. By masking the advantages on these special tokens, the method prevents the teacher from directly penalizing termination behavior. It is a small intervention, but it targets a very specific instability in long-form reasoning distillation.

Overall, SimpleOPD is not a new OPD framework so much as a compact recipe that combines cross-tokenizer alignment, length control, and termination stabilization. The paper also emphasizes that the gains are obtained without supervised warm-starting on teacher trajectories; the transfer is achieved primarily through the on-policy process itself.21:T6f7,The experiments distill from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 into multiple shorter-context students from different families, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2-Preview, GLM-4.7, and Gemma-4. The headline result is on ProofBench: Intern-S2-Preview improves from 34.0 to 55.2, a gain of 21.2 points, surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro and approaching substantially stronger reasoning models. The paper highlights that the strongest gains appear on natural-language mathematical proof tasks, which suggests the transfer is not merely about answer formatting but about longer-horizon proof organization.

The distilled students also improve on science-oriented benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, despite being trained only on mathematical reasoning data. That indicates some degree of out-of-domain generalization in the transferred reasoning behavior. The ablations are especially informative. Removing the reference KL causes response lengths to grow rapidly, truncation rates to rise, and optimization to become unstable. Removing termination-token masking makes the model much less willing to end its thinking or final response. Increasing the distillation length can further help long-form reasoning, but only when the resulting trajectories still fit within the student’s context budget. The paper also reports that larger teacher-student gaps generally require stronger KL coefficients; otherwise the student is pulled toward a long-trajectory distribution it cannot sustain.

The most useful evidence is not just the benchmark gains but the training-dynamics analysis: response-length distributions, truncation behavior, and the role of termination tokens. Those analyses explain why naive OPD fails and why the proposed recipe remains stable across heterogeneous model families.22:T402,The method has two components. ARC, or Advantage Regularization via Conditioning, attaches a strategy instruction during training, samples multiple rollouts within that strategy-conditioned class, computes group-relative advantages only within the class, and updates the policy with a hybrid reward plus entropy regularization. At inference time, the strategy instruction is removed, so the policy must choose its interaction strategy on its own. This differs from hint-based RL: the auxiliary instruction is not meant to reveal the solution or improve exploration, but to make rollout comparison fair. INTER3 is the interaction substrate. Plain text is treated as latent reasoning, `<answer>` spans are streamed to the user, and tool calls remain structured internal actions. This allows progress updates, clarification, alignment checks, interruptions, and mid-execution steering to be represented explicitly. The authors build INTER3-86K from real online traces, curated public data, and synthetic diversified trajectories.23:T4be,The approach adapts neuroscience-style functional localizers and network recruitment analyses to LLM circuits:
- Task suite: 46 tasks spanning four domains, used to elicit domain-consistent activation patterns rather than to optimize a single benchmark.
- Module identification: contrastive analyses over internal representations (e.g., residual stream components, MLP/attention outputs, or discovered directions) to find units/subnetworks selectively engaged by a domain.
- Segregation tests: quantify overlap vs separation between domain-selective sets, checking whether domain modules are approximately distinct and consistent across tasks/prompts.
- Causal tests (if included in the full paper): ablation/patching/activation interventions to show domain-specific performance dependence, moving from correlation to causation.
- Brain analogy: map discovered LLM modules onto coarse human networks (language, Multiple Demand, Theory of Mind, intuitive physics) to argue convergent functional decomposition.
Key difference from prior ‘feature neuron’ work is the attempt to characterize domain-level functional organization with a unified task-contrast methodology and explicit overlap/segregation evaluation.24:T499,The paradox is traced to two distinct mismatches. First, coverage mismatch: Power Sampling applies exponentiation per-trajectory, which amplifies the weight of a single high-probability wrong trajectory while suppressing the collective support of multiple moderate-probability trajectories leading to the same correct answer, flipping the final majority decision. Second, dose mismatch: a fixed sharpening exponent α induces highly variable distribution shifts across different prompts, ranging from mild adjustment to near-complete distribution collapse on hard problems. The proposed Relative-Rank SoftSat fix addresses both issues: it replaces raw likelihood with per-prompt relative rank normalized to [0,1] to equalize sharpening strength across problems, and uses a saturating weighting function that boosts moderately ranked trajectories while capping gains for top-ranked trajectories, preventing a small number of dominant paths from absorbing excessive probability mass. An importance-weighted aggregation implementation is also provided that reuses the same N candidate trajectories from base model sampling, matching the compute budget of vanilla Self-Consistency.25:T5cf,The core method is a preregistered multiverse analysis. Instead of choosing one analysis pipeline, the authors enumerate a large grid of defensible specifications, with every setting taken from published implementations rather than invented for the study.

A specification includes multiple analytic axes: discovery objective, ablation operator, corruption distribution, evaluation metric, size threshold, prompt variant, seed, and granularity. The testbed is GPT-2 small on the indirect object identification task, using factorized-graph circuit discovery. Each specification returns a circuit C(s), a subset of model edges.

The key move is that the paper does not stop at graph overlap. It introduces a deterministic claim map φ that converts each discovered circuit into a structured Annex IV-style statement. This shifts the evaluation target from “do the circuits look similar?” to “would the resulting regulatory explanation be the same?” The paper then defines flip rate, modal share π*, and conditional within-group variants, and proposes a filability criterion: evidence is filable at tolerance α only if π* ≥ 1 − α.

A second methodological contribution is statistical. When different specifications produce circuits of different sizes, pooled stability statistics can reverse or mislead. The authors therefore analyze pooled results, size-controlled results, and claim mappings with size information removed. That point generalizes beyond this specific task.26:T56c,The experimental sweep is large: 15,840 preregistered specifications on GPT-2 small and IOI, of which 7,561 produced a claim through the deterministic map.

The main findings are:
1. Claim instability is high. The reported flip rate is 0.7316. With 9 observed claim classes, the theoretical ceiling is 0.8889, so the observed value is 82.3% of the maximum possible. In practical terms, two defensible specifications often yield different regulatory statements.
2. The modal share is too low to satisfy the proposed filability criterion under reasonable tolerances, meaning current circuit evidence would struggle to qualify as stable filing material.
3. The instability survives aggressive controls. It remains even when one analytic axis is standardized, when circuit size is removed from the claim map, and when circuit size is held fixed.
4. The discovered circuits are not random noise. At fixed circuit size, they are more stable than a size-matched random null: 0.2746 versus 0.4230. But functional instability is still substantial: 0.3972 pooled and 0.4777 for the smallest circuits. Cohen’s kappa is only 0.0146, indicating that circuits found under different specifications are functionally almost uncorrelated.

Taken together, the evidence supports a restrained conclusion: circuit discovery contains signal, but in its current form it does not provide audit-grade stability.27:T514,Do not read this one from the abstract alone. Start with the introduction and especially the section titled “What this paper is not claiming.” The authors are careful about scope, and that section prevents the common misreading that the paper is claiming mechanistic interpretability is useless.

Then read the formalism closely. Three objects matter most: the specification tuple, the deterministic claim map φ, and the filability criterion. The novelty is less about a new discovery algorithm and more about connecting analytic variation to the stability of downstream explanatory claims.

In the experiments, prioritize:
1. the seven analytic axes and where each setting comes from;
2. flip rate, modal share, and within-group stability metrics;
3. robustness checks with one axis fixed, circuit size controlled, and size removed from claims;
4. comparisons to random nulls and the kappa analysis.

If the goal is only the main message, about 45 minutes is enough. 下，最优重复次数随模型规模温和增加；这与固定数据量比较下的结论相反。第二，不同领域之间的最优重复次数差异较大，并且与最终 validation loss 强负相关：validation loss 更低的领域可以从更多重复中受益，validation loss 更高的领域更早出现重复带来的退化。第三，在测试的唯一高质量 token 比例范围内，最优重复次数对唯一数据量不太敏感。这个结果给出一个实用流程：可以在较小 proxy model 上保持相同 TPP，任取一个有代表性的高质量数据比例 sweep 重复次数；如果某个重复次数在小模型上没有过拟合，那么迁移到同 TPP 的更大模型时通常偏保守。提供的正文片段没有列出具体模型参数量、token 规模、表格数值和各领域最优重复次数，因此不应额外推断具体数字。","reading_guide":"建议精读。先看 Figure 1，它把固定数据量和固定 TPP 两种设置的差异讲得很清楚，也是论文最重要的概念入口。然后看实验设置中 mixture 构造、领域数据重复方式、唯一数据比例控制、validation loss 定义和过拟合判据。结果部分重点看三类图表：最优重复次数随模型规模变化的曲线、不同领域 validation loss 与最优重复次数的关系、唯一领域数据量变化下最优重复次数是否稳定。真正值得深挖的是 proxy model 迁移原则：小模型 sweep 不是为了精确预测大模型最优点，而是为了找到在同 TPP scaling 下相对安全的重复下界。这个结论如果成立，会减少大模型数据配比搜索成本。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical data-mixture problem in LLM pretraining. As models scale, the training-token budget usually grows with the tokens-per-parameter ratio, but high-quality domain data does not scale as easily as general web data. Without repetition, its mixture share shrinks. With too much repetition, the model may overfit. The paper asks how many times domain data should be repeated under fixed-TPP scaling.","importance":"The contribution is directly relevant to pretraining data recipes. Prior studies often compare model sizes while holding the total data budget fixed, which leads to the conclusion that larger models should repeat less because they overfit repeated data sooner. This paper argues that practical LLM scaling is closer to fixed TPP, where the token budget grows with model size. Under that setting, the optimal repetition count mildly increases with model size. This changes how one should allocate scarce high-quality code, math, Wikipedia, or medical data in large-scale pretraining mixtures.","technical_approach":"The paper treats repetition as domain-specific reuse inside a pretraining mixture, rather than multi-epoch training on a single dataset. The key variables are model size, total training tokens, TPP, the amount of unique domain data, the repetition count, and the amount of general data used to fill the remaining budget. The experiments cover four high-quality domains: code, math, Wikipedia, and medical data. For each domain, the authors vary the amount of unique data and the repetition count while keeping the total training budget fixed for each model size. This isolates the benefit of increasing the domain share from the overfitting risk caused by repeated examples. A central distinction is between fixed-data scaling and fixed-TPP scaling. With fixed data, larger models see the same repeated examples and overfit earlier. With fixed TPP, the token budget grows with model size, and repeated domain data is embedded in a larger training stream, which makes higher repetition counts viable.","experiments":"The main empirical findings are clear. First, at fixed TPP, the optimal repetition count increases mildly with model size, reversing the trend observed under fixed-data comparisons. Second, the optimal repetition count differs across domains and is strongly negatively correlated with final validation loss. Domains with lower validation loss can tolerate and benefit from more repetition, while higher-loss domains overfit earlier. Third, within the tested range, the optimal repetition count is largely insensitive to the amount of unique high-quality data. This leads to a practical procedure: sweep repetition counts on a smaller proxy model at the same TPP using any representative fraction of unique domain tokens, then use the safe region as a conservative guide for the larger target model. The provided text does not expose exact parameter counts, token budgets, tables, or per-domain optimal repetition values, so those numbers should not be inferred.","reading_guide":"Read carefully. Start with Figure 1, which explains why fixed-data and fixed-TPP comparisons lead to different conclusions. Then inspect the experimental setup: how the mixture is constructed, how domain repetition is implemented, how unique-domain-token fractions are controlled, and how validation loss is used to detect overfitting. In the results, focus on three relationships: optimal repetition versus model size, optimal repetition versus domain validation loss, and stability of the optimum under changes in unique data amount. The most useful idea to examine is the proxy-transfer rule. The small model sweep is not meant to predict the exact optimum for the large model; it is meant to find a conservative safe repetition range under matched TPP.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻把 domain repetition 纳入 mixture sweep；低 loss 领域可多重复这一点会改变常规去重直觉。","en":"Add domain repetition to the mixture sweep immediately; the low-loss-domains-can-repeat-more result pushes against the usual dedup-first instinct."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jingwei Li","Xinran Gu","Rui Dai","Xintong Hao","Chengyin Xu","Yan Wu","Shuran Zheng","Jingzhao Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["pretraining-data","data-mixture","data-repetition","domain-data","scaling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14071","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.14071","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}}]},{"id":"industry","emoji":"🔥","name":"行业动态与观点","description":"重大产品/模型发布、开源发布、行业事件、核心研究员观点（注意：推理加速/注意力优化等技术论文不算行业动态）","papers":[{"id":"2608.13675","title":"From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models","abstract":"Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T17:07:37Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14492.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Muon优化器在LLM全参数预训练上表现优于AdamW，但无法直接适配当前主流的参数高效微调方法LoRA——LoRA的低秩参数化无法实现全层权重更新的正交化，现有近似方案（如分别对A/B矩阵做正交化）破坏了Muon保持更新谱平坦的核心设计，部分方案依赖SVD/QR等硬件不友好的矩阵分解，计算开销高。","importance":"该工作解决了Muon优化器落地到LoRA微调、ReLoRA类持续预训练场景的核心适配问题，提出的sMuon不需要昂贵的矩阵分解，和现有硬件的矩阵乘指令高度适配，同时保留了Muon正交化更新的核心优势，对Muon生态的推广、尤其是低资源微调、基于ReLoRA的增量预训练场景有实际应用价值。","technical_approach":"该工作从Muon的原始目标（谱范数下的最速下降，让权重更新的奇异值均匀化）出发，对低秩约束下的Muon目标做一阶线性近似，忽略二阶小项，推导出闭式解：本质是先对全层的低秩更新梯度做正交化，再投影到LoRA的低秩子空间上，避免了现有per-factor Muon分别正交化A/B矩阵导致的全层更新谱畸变问题。进一步推导了等价的纯矩阵乘实现，完全不需要SVD/QR分解，优化器状态占用比AdamW更低，速度和现有per-factor Muon相当，比基于黎曼优化的低秩Muon快30%左右。","experiments":"该工作在4个基座模型（3个AdamW预训练、1个Muon预训练）、11个SFT基准上测试，sMuon在Muon预训练的Moonlight-16B-A3B模型上表现最优，在11个任务中的6个取得最高准确率；在ReLoRA增量预训练实验中，sMuon取得了最低的最终验证困惑度，和黎曼优化方案的最终性能持平，但训练速度快30%；所有Muon类LoRA优化器的表现均优于AdamW LoRA基线。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看引言部分对现有LoRA+Muon适配方案缺陷的总结，以及方法部分的一阶近似推导思路，理解为什么直接对A/B矩阵分别做正交化不符合Muon的原始目标；实现部分的纯矩阵乘优化和实验结果可以快速浏览，预计阅读时间25分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The Muon optimizer delivers consistent gains over AdamW for full-parameter LLM pretraining, but does not natively work with LoRA, the dominant parameter-efficient fine-tuning method: the low-rank parameterization of LoRA makes it mathematically impossible to orthogonalize full-layer weight updates as required by standard Muon. Existing approximate approaches either break Muon's core spectral flattening property (e.g. orthogonalizing LoRA A/B factors separately) or rely on hardware-unfriendly SVD/QR decompositions that add significant compute overhead.","importance":"This work resolves the core compatibility gap between Muon and LoRA, enabling Muon's spectral normalization benefits to be applied to PEFT and ReLoRA-style continual pretraining workloads. The proposed sMuon optimizer relies exclusively on matmul operations that map efficiently to modern hardware, while preserving Muon's core update properties, lowering the barrier to adopting Muon across both fine-tuning and incremental pretraining pipelines.","technical_approach":"Starting from Muon's original objective — steepest descent under the spectral norm, which equalizes singular values of the weight update — the work derives a first-order linear approximation of the Muon objective under low-rank constraints, dropping small second-order terms to produce a closed-form solution. The solution effectively orthogonalizes the full-layer gradient of the LoRA update before projecting it onto the low-rank LoRA subspace, avoiding the spectral distortion introduced by per-factor Muon approaches that orthogonalize A and B matrices independently. An equivalent, compute-efficient reformulation is provided that uses only matrix multiplications, eliminating SVD/QR calls entirely, with lower optimizer state overhead than AdamW.","experiments":"Evaluated across 4 base models (3 AdamW-pretrained, 1 Muon-pretrained) on 11 SFT benchmarks, sMuon delivers the strongest performance on the Muon-pretrained Moonlight-16B-A3B model, achieving top accuracy on 6 out of 11 tasks. In ReLoRA incremental pretraining experiments, sMuon matches the lowest final validation loss achieved by Riemannian optimization-based low-rank Muon, while running ~30% faster. All tested Muon-based LoRA optimizers outperform the AdamW LoRA baseline.","reading_guide":"Skim for core design insights. However, existing dLLMs can suffer from unreliable predictions in early denoising stages under aggressive parallelism strategies, leading to errors that can propagate to later stages. To tackle this issue, we present Consistency Forcing (CForce) for dLLMs, a distillation method to force the mask predictions of early stages to align with those of later stages. CForce train","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T03:52:33Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13925.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 diffusion LLM 在高并行解码下的一个核心瓶颈：早期去噪阶段的预测不可靠，尤其在 aggressive threshold decoding 时，模型会一次性提交很多 token，但这些 token 当时可用上下文还很弱，错误一旦提交就会向后传播，导致速度上去了、质量却明显掉。作者认为，dLLM 的加速问题不能只理解为“减少 denoising step 数”，更关键的是“让早期阶段在高并行承诺下也更可靠”。这个问题在 edit-capable dLLM 上更突出，因为后期 token-to-token refinement 阶段拥有更丰富上下文，也可能修正早期草稿；如果训练时没有显式利用这种后期信息去约束早期预测，推理时就会出现训练-推理错位：模型被要求在最难、信息最少的阶段做高置信提交，但训练目标并没有专门优化这一点。","importance":"对新型语言建模范式研究，这篇论文的价值很明确。第一，它把 dLLM 的解码效率问题重新表述为 early-stage reliability 问题，而不是单纯的采样步数问题。这是一个更贴近实际系统瓶颈的视角，因为高 TPF（tokens per forward）只有在早期预测足够稳时才真正有意义。第二，它提出的训练方式不是依赖外部 teacher 或构造的 privileged trajectory，而是沿着模型自己的 self-rollout threshold-decoding 轨迹做相邻阶段一致性蒸馏，这直接改善了 training-inference alignment。对于 diffusion LM，这一点比单纯做离线蒸馏更关键。第三，它把 consistency model 的思想移植到离散 masked diffusion LM，并针对 still-masked positions 设计了 confidence-adaptive KL，再配合 reveal-boundary 的 CE anchor 和 curriculum。这个组合不是概念性嫁接，而是紧扣 dLLM 的具体误差来源。第四，结果显示在 edit-capable LLaDA2.1-mini 上，速度和质量可以同时提升；在 non-edit LLaDA2.0-mini 上，则呈现清晰的 speed-quality trade-off。也就是说，这个方法既揭示了 edit-capable dLLM 的额外可蒸馏结构，也说明其收益边界与模型是否支持后期修正密切相关。","technical_approach":"$a","experiments":"实验最关键的结果出现在两个模型上。对 edit-capable 的 LLaDA2.1-mini，CForce 把平均 TPF 从 6.94 提升到 9.08，同时平均准确率从 85.57 提升到 86.41。这个结果很重要，因为它不是常见的“更快但略差”，而是在作者的设置下实现了速度和质量同时改善，说明后期 T2T refinement 确实为早期阶段提供了有价值的蒸馏信号。对 non-edit 的 LLaDA2.0-mini，CForce 把平均 TPF 从 3.60 提升到 6.42，但这里更像显式的 speed-quality trade-off：在激进解码下 few-step accuracy 更好，在固定 TPF 预算下也优于基线，但不像 edit-capable 模型那样自然获得双升。这和方法动机一致，因为 non-edit 模型后期状态没有 T2T 修正带来的额外信息增益。\n\n从全文叙述看，实验还包含几类关键分析。第一类是不同解码预算或不同 threshold aggressiveness 下的质量曲线，验证 CForce 的收益主要集中在高并行、少步数区域，而不是所有设置都平均提升。第二类是损失项消融，比较 CAD、纯 forward KL、纯 reverse KL、以及是否加 CE anchor。这里最值得关注的是 CAD 是否在高置信和低置信区域都更稳，以及 CE anchor 对 token commitment 的帮助有多大。第三类是 curriculum 的作用，通常会体现在训练稳定性或最终 few-step accuracy 上。第四类是 edit-capable 与 non-edit 模型的对比，这部分实际上回答了一个更深的问题：一致性蒸馏到底是在利用“更多上下文”，还是在利用“后期可修正的更优轨迹状态”。从主结果看，两者都有，但后者在 LLaDA2.1 上更明显。\n\n如果精读实验，建议特别留意作者如何定义 accuracy、TPF 的统计方式、block diffusion 设置、以及 self-rollout trajectory 的采样与缓存成本。因为这类方法在论文里看起来只是多一个蒸馏目标，落到系统实现时，trajectory collection 和 stage partition 的开销会直接决定它是否适合大规模训练。","reading_guide":"建议先读 Introduction 和 Preliminaries，把 block diffusion、M2T、T2T refinement、threshold decoding 这些对象关系理顺。若不先搞清楚 edit-capable dLLM 与普通 masked diffusion 的区别，很容易低估这篇方法为什么对 LLaDA2.1 更有效。\n\n然后重点读方法章节里关于 self-rollout trajectory、stage partition、CAD、CE anchor 的定义。这里有三个关键问题要带着看：为什么必须用 self-rollout 而不是 teacher rollout；为什么按 reveal-count 切 stage 而不是 native step；为什么 KL 需要按 later-stage confidence 自适应，而不是固定一种方向。理论分析部分建议一起看，因为它把 CAD 和 CE 的必要性讲得比较清楚。\n\n实验部分优先看两张主图和主表：LLaDA2.1-mini 上 TPF/accuracy 同时提升，LLaDA2.0-mini 上 speed-quality trade-off。接着看消融，尤其是 CAD 对比 forward/reverse KL、是否加 CE anchor、以及 curriculum 的影响。如果你做 diffusion LM 或 any-order LM，这篇最值得深挖的点不是单个数字，而是它提出的训练-推理对齐思路：让模型在自己真实会走到的早期状态上，向自己更后期、更有信息的状态学习。泛读大约 40 分钟可抓住主线；若要复现或迁移到别的 dLLM，至少需要 1.5 到 2 小时把 trajectory 构造、损失定义和解码设置读细。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central bottleneck in diffusion LLM decoding: under aggressive parallel decoding, early denoising stages make unreliable predictions, and once many tokens are committed too early, those errors propagate through the rest of the trajectory. The authors argue that dLLM acceleration should not be framed only as reducing the number of denoising steps. The harder and more relevant problem is improving early-stage reliability when the model is forced to reveal many tokens with limited context. This is especially acute for edit-capable dLLMs, where later token-to-token refinement stages have richer context and can revise earlier drafts, yet standard training does not explicitly teach early predictions to align with those stronger later states.","importance":"$b","technical_approach":"$c","experiments":"$d","reading_guide":"$e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应优先看；dLLM 的瓶颈不是并行步数本身，而是早期轨迹错配，值得在内部 LLaDA/MDLM ablation 复现。","en":"Prioritize this: the dLLM bottleneck is early-trajectory mismatch, not just step count, and it is worth reproducing in internal LLaDA/MDLM ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Ant Group","Affiliation:Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Affiliation:Ant Group{renyj"],"authors":["Yuji Ren","Chenkai Xu","Zhuocheng Gong","Jianguo Li","Zhijie Deng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-LM","parallel-decoding","consistency-distillation","masked-language-modeling","train-inference-alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13925","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.13925","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.13570","title":"Think in Latent, Explain in Language: Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning","abstract":"Latent reasoning has emerged as a powerful alternative to text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT), offering significant gains in computational efficiency by compressing verbose reasoning into compact embeddings. However, compressing reasoning into the latent space renders the thinking opaque, hindering its interpretability. Current methods present a stark trade-off: they either function as unexplainable ''black boxes'' (e.g., Coconut), where the latent reasoning is not human-readable, or rely on separ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T16:11:00Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13570.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作要解决 latent reasoning 的两个直接障碍：连续隐空间推理比文本 CoT 更省 token，但推理过程不可读；如果另训一个解释器，又会带来额外参数，并且解释可能和真正参与求解的隐状态脱节。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在建模目标，而不是训练规模。SELR 把“用 latent token 求解”和“把 latent token 还原成语言解释”放进同一个模型、同一次训练目标里，给 latent reasoning 提供了一个较干净的监督接口。它说明连续思维 token 不一定只能作为黑箱中间态，也可以被约束到可被语言解码的区域。对 VLM 更有参考意义，因为视觉输入本来就是连续 embedding，latent reasoning 可以减少从视觉连续表示到离散文本 CoT 的往返损耗。不过当前工作更接近 约束连续推理后能给出正确答案；CoT Loss 要求同一个模型把自己的 latent thought 解码成人类可读的推理步骤。这个设计和 Coconut 类方法的区别是，它不只追求隐空间求解效率；和 post-hoc decoder 类方法的区别是，它不引入独立解释器，而是让 reasoner 自己承担 latent-to-language 翻译。训练策略上，工作比较了单阶段训练和多阶段 curriculum：先在较简单的文本任务上做消融，寻找 latent token 数量、解释损失、答案损失、训练阶段切换等配置，再迁移到 VLM。","experiments":"实验分两层。第一层在轻量 LLM 和小规模文本推理数据上做训练策略消融，用来确认 CoT Loss 是否能同时提供监督和解释能力，以及单阶段、多阶段训练的取舍。第二层迁移到 VLM，使用 Qwen2.5-VL 作为基础模型，在多个视觉语言推理 benchmark 上评估准确率和生成 token 数。全文描述的主要结论是：SELR fine-tuning 后在若干 VLM benchmark 上同时减少生成 token、提高分数，并且能够把连续 thought 解码成语言解释。提供内容中没有展开具体表格数值，因此精读时需要直接核对实验表和消融图，尤其是 token saving 与 accuracy gain 是否在不同任务上稳定。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Introduction，抓住 latent reasoning 的监督缺口和可解释性缺口。再看 Approach 中 Answer Loss、CoT Loss 和 hidden-state feedback 的定义，这是主要信息。实验部分优先看训练策略消融，而不是只看最终 benchmark；关键问题是 CoT Loss 是否真的改善 latent state 的可用性，还是只学会了事后复述。VLM 部分建议重点看 Qwen2.5-VL 的接入方式、latent token 预算、解释样例，以及 token 数减少是否伴随答案质量提升。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The work targets a concrete weakness of latent reasoning: continuous thought tokens can reduce verbose text-based CoT, but they make the reasoning path opaque. Adding a separate decoder improves readability, yet adds parameters and may explain a representation that is not tightly coupled to the actual solver.","importance":"The main value for pretraining researchers is in the objective design, not in scale. SELR treats latent solving and latent-to-language explanation as two tasks of the same model. This gives latent reasoning a more usable supervision channel and suggests that continuous thought states can be trained to stay in a region that remains decodable into language. The VLM angle is also relevant: visual features are already continuous, so latent reasoning may avoid some unnecessary conversions between continuous visual representations and discrete textual CoT. The current work is closer to reasoning-oriented fine-tuning than to foundation pretraining, but the training signal is useful for thinking about future latent-token objectives.","technical_approach":"SELR feeds the previous hidden state back as the next input embedding, creating continuous thought tokens instead of always sampling discrete text tokens. Training uses two losses. The Answer Loss makes the latent reasoning path useful for producing the final answer. The CoT Loss trains the same model to decode its own latent thoughts into readable reasoning steps. This differs from Coconut-style latent reasoning, which leaves the latent trajectory largely unreadable, and from post-hoc decoder approaches, where the explainer is separate from the reasoner. The paper studies both single-stage training and multi-stage curriculum training, first on small text-only reasoning setups and then in a VLM setting.","experiments":"The experiments are organized as a strategy search followed by VLM validation. Small LLM experiments test the effects of the explanation loss, answer loss, latent thought configuration, and curriculum choices. The selected recipe is then applied to Qwen2.5-VL. The reported result is a simultaneous reduction in generated tokens and improved scores on multiple VLM benchmarks, while the model can decode its own continuous thoughts into natural-language explanations. The provided text does not include the detailed numerical tables, so the exact magnitude of accuracy gains and token savings should be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. Read the introduction to understand the supervision and interpretability gaps in latent reasoning. Then focus on the approach section, especially the hidden-state feedback mechanism and the two-loss objective. In the experiments, prioritize the ablations over the headline benchmark numbers. The most important question is whether the CoT Loss shapes useful latent states, rather than merely producing plausible explanations after the fact. The core problem is how to extract more task-relevant structure from a large model, under the constraints of no training and no neuron-level semantic alignment, to deliver larger performance gains to a small model with a tiny injection weight.","importance":"For pretraining research, this work offers three key insights. First, it reveals the organization of capabilities in large models: cross-scale transfer at small injection ratios behaves more like adding a \"concentrated donor extract\" than neuron-wise transplantation, indicating task-relevant capabilities are concentrated in specific activation subsets. This provides new evidence for understanding emergent capabilities and modularity in pretrained models. Second, the training-free fusion method provides a low-cost way to enhance small base models, and can serve as a diagnostic tool for pretraining checkpoints to verify capability correspondence across scales. Third, it complements traditional model merging and distillation paradigms with a training-free knowledge transfer path.","technical_approach":"The proposed Activation-Prune-Merge (APM) framework has three steps. First, activation mapping: run the large donor model on task data, collect activation statistics over a specified token range, and build task-conditioned activation maps. Second, target-shape pruning: select salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons based on activation maps, pruning the large model to exactly match the architecture of the small recipient. Third, micro-injection: inject the pruned donor slice into the original small model with a very small interpolation coefficient, preserving the small model's dominant role and inference cost. The key difference from IM is replacing fixed front-parameter truncation with task-relevant activation-guided selection.","experiments":"Experiments cover reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification tasks, with baselines including the original 3B recipient model and Intersection-Merge (IM). Key results include three points. Second, at small injection ratios, activation-guided selection consistently outperforms fixed truncation, supporting the \"concentration transfer\" hypothesis — small-ratio fusion effectiveness depends on task relevance of donor information, not strict neuron semantic alignment. Third, sequential multi-stage fusion (repeated small-ratio injections) remains effective; APM-fused models can continue to benefit from subsequent donor injections.","reading_guide":"For skimming, focus on the core problem and the \"concentration transfer\" perspective in the Introduction (≈10 minutes), plus the three-step APM pipeline and comparison with IM in the Method section (≈10 minutes). squares 闭式求解 dequantization 参数，最后选择重构误差最小的候选。理论部分将 QAT 收敛界分成初始化项、minibatch noise 项和 loss-aware reconstruction error 项，其中只有最后一项由重构映射控制，正好对应 QUASAR 的优化目标；在额外 PL 条件下，这个误差也影响最终量化模型的 BitDistiller。文中报告 INT4 和 INT3 下 evaluation loss 至少降低 10%，INT2 下至少降低 29%。在 NVFP4 设置下，相比 Standard QAT evaluation loss 约降低 30%。这些结果说明收益主要来自更好的训练轨迹，而不是只在训练结束时做一次静态重构。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25 分钟。先看 reconstruction error 为什么是可控项。实验部分优先看 INT4/INT3/INT2、NVFP4，以及与 LSQ、Denoising QAT、BitDistiller 的对比和消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"QUASAR targets a structural optimization mismatch in QAT. The forward pass and loss are computed with quantized-dequantized weights r, while the optimizer updates latent full-precision weights w. Because the straight-through estimator uses gradients with respect to r as a surrogate for gradients with respect to w, QAT can follow a suboptimal trajectory and converge to a higher loss floor than full-precision training.","importance":"The main relevance is native low-precision training and the interface between training and deployment. The work reframes QAT as a continual loss-aware reconstruction problem rather than a fixed quantization conversion problem. This is useful for LLM settings where FP4, INT4, or lower-precision serving is expected, and where PTQ becomes brittle for reasoning, agentic, or long-context models.","technical_approach":"$f","experiments":"The reported results show lower training and evaluation loss across bit widths and model families. In quantization-aware distillation of Qwen3-4B-Thinking, QUASAR outperforms Standard QAT, LSQ, Denoising QAT, and BitDistiller at every tested bit width. The evaluation loss reduction is at least 10% for INT4 and INT3, at least 29% for INT2, and about 30% for NVFP4 relative to Standard QAT. The gains are consistent with the proposed mechanism: improving the reconstruction during training changes the optimization trajectory, rather than only repairing a frozen model at the end.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25 minutes. 缺口。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses KV cache memory pressure in long-context inference. The key question is how to compress the cache at high compression ratios while preserving the attention output, rather than merely minimizing reconstruction error on the cached tensors.","importance":"This is mainly an inference-efficiency contribution, but it is relevant to long-context LLM research. The useful idea is that KV cache compression should be output-aware: an error is harmful only to the extent that it propagates through queries, attention, and the output projection. This gives a cleaner way to reason about why some model families and GQA configurations are more sensitive to cache compression than others.","technical_approach":"The method formulates KV cache compression as transform coding. AATC applies a whitening-based per-layer transform to decorrelate cache features, then performs bit allocation in the transform domain. Unlike variance-only or reconstruction-error criteria, AATC weights distortion by attention-aware terms, including query-related q_c^2 factors and output-projection-related W_Oc factors, derived from an output-error analysis. The implementation keeps the first s=4 tokens and the most recent w=128 tokens in full precision. Once the recent-token window is full, it quantizes the oldest 16 tokens and refills the window. Calibration uses WikiText-2 for the transform, and 256 sequences of length 2048 for rate-distortion calibration, split evenly between FineWeb and OpenR1-Math-220k. The var-only ablation removes the attention-aware terms and allocates bits based only on quantization-error variance, making it a per-layer analogue of a KVTC-style reconstruction criterion.","experiments":"The main evaluation covers Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, with comparisons to KIVI, PALU, KVQuant, AATC var-only, and full AATC. At 5.82× compression, AATC is statistically indistinguishable from the FP16 baseline within 2 standard errors across all 18 reported cells. For Llama, the KV cache memory drops from 1.07 GB in FP16 to 184 MB. Qwen is much harder to compress: KIVI falls to 0.351 on LongBench and below 0.28 on several RULER settings, while AATC reaches 0.567 on LongBench versus 0.574 for FP16, and 0.715 on RULER-32k versus 0.720 for FP16. On Qwen MMLU-Pro math, AATC scores 0.710, compared with 0.674 for AATC var-only and 0.478 for KVQuant. At the more aggressive 2-bit, roughly 7× setting, the attention-aware allocation becomes more important: full AATC remains close to baseline on long-context RULER, while the var-only variant degrades as context length increases.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 20 minutes. The most heavily loaded rank stalls global execution due to skewed routing distributions, directly increasing latency. While offline expert placement can alleviate persistent imbalance, practical multi-task serving workloads exhibit layer- and batch-dependent routing dynamics, making online load balancing indispensable. Existing approaches rely on routing s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T11:30:39Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14205.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是分布式 MoE 推理里的在线负载均衡时机问题。已有在线方法通常要等当前层 router 真正跑完，拿到 token→expert 的路由分布后，才能决定是否迁移专家权重或调整 expert-device mapping。这样一来，迁移动作只能发生在 routing 之后、expert 计算之前，直接压在关键路径上。作者抓住的核心矛盾是：负载信息获取和专家映射调整被串行依赖绑死了，导致在线均衡虽然能减轻偏载，却常常把迁移开销又加回了延迟里。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究，尤其是 MoE 系统和 serving，这篇的价值在于它把‘在线均衡’从 router 之后挪到了 router 之前，而且不改模型输出。它不是训练侧的 load balancing loss，也不是静态 expert placement，而是一个推理系统层面的新切入点：如果能在真正路由前用很轻的代价预测即将到来的工作负载，就可以把专家迁移与前序计算重叠，减少关键路径上的同步等待。这个思路对大规模 MoE serving 很实用，也给训练/推理协同设计一个提醒：router 的中间表示可能比最终路由结果更早暴露出足够的负载信号。","technical_approach":"方法叫 FreeBalance，核心是 pre-routing online balancing。作者观察到，某一层 MoE router 的输入表示，与前一层残差流里的表示高度相关，因此可以在真正执行当前 router 之前，把前一层的 residual representation 喂给对应 router，得到一个对即将发生的 expert workload 的近似预测。这个预测不用于改写模型路由，只用于系统调度。拿到预测后的负载分布，系统提前决定 expert-device mapping 的调整，并立刻开始专家权重迁移，让迁移过程与当前层前面的计算，主要是 attention 计算，尽量重叠。为了避免‘为了均衡而过度迁移’，作者又加了一个 cost model，根据可重叠的时间窗口限制迁移专家数量。也就是说，FreeBalance 不是盲目追求最均匀负载，而是在负载改善和迁移成本之间做受约束优化。一个重要设计点是：实际 MoE 执行仍然使用原始 router 的真实 top-k 结果，因此模型输出不变，收益完全来自系统层调度。","experiments":"从摘要和正文可见的关键结果有三项。第一，FreeBalance 将 的关键路径延迟。论文强调这种收益在长序列推理下更明显，因为 attention 本身提供了更大的可重叠窗口。就方法定位而言，这些数字说明作者不是单纯做了更好的 placement，而是确实把 balancing overhead 从 critical path 上挪走了一部分。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看引言和 Figure 1/2，弄清楚作者到底在改什么：不是改 model，判断它如何根据可重叠窗口限制迁移规模。实验部分建议优先看三类结果：负载分布改善、端到端延迟、以及不同序列长度下的收益变化。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要贡献；如果要继续深读，值得追问的是预测误差在不同任务混合负载下有多稳定，以及频繁迁移专家权重对缓存、带宽和内存碎片的长期影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a timing bottleneck in online load balancing for distributed MoE inference. Existing online methods wait until the current router finishes, observe the actual token-to-expert distribution, and only then decide whether to migrate expert weights or adjust expert-device placement. That makes migration happen between routing and expert execution, directly on the critical path. The paper identifies the root issue as a sequential dependency: workload information is acquired too late for balancing to be hidden behind other computation.","importance":"For foundation-model systems, especially MoE serving, the main contribution is not a new training-side balancing loss or a better static placement heuristic. It is a systems idea: move online balancing from post-routing to pre-routing without changing model outputs. If upcoming expert workloads can be predicted cheaply before the router actually runs, expert migration can overlap with preceding computation, mainly attention, instead of extending end-to-end latency. This is practically relevant for large-scale MoE deployment and conceptually useful because it shows that intermediate representations may expose enough routing signal earlier than the final routing decision itself.","technical_approach":"$10","experiments":"The main reported numbers are clear. FreeBalance reduces the max-to-mean rank load ratio by 32.8%, indicating a substantial reduction in straggler severity across the expert-parallel group. More importantly, it reduces end-to-end latency by 13.1%, which shows that the balancing improvement translates into real serving gains rather than just nicer load statistics. The paper also reports that it hides the online balancing overhead of an average of 5.1 experts per layer; if that migration had stayed on the critical path, it would have accounted for 8.5% of critical-path latency. The paper further argues that the benefit is especially meaningful for long-sequence inference, where attention provides a larger overlap window for migration.","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and the main system figures to understand what is being changed: not the router, not the experts, but the timing of online balancing. After that, inspect the cost model and how it constrains migration under limited overlap opportunity. In the experiments, prioritize the load-balance metrics, end-to-end latency, and the results across different sequence lengths. This should take about 20–30 minutes.
