To constrain additional model storage overhead, several existing methods gate layer selection by goodness-of-fit, retaining only those layers whose compensation yields a positive residual fit score and discarding the rest. In this paper, we show that, under the low-bit W4A4 setting, this gating mechanism fails to distinguish poorly predictable quantization error from numerica","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T10:00:36Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14149.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 W4A4 训练后量化里一个被长期低估的问题：闭式残差补偿在一些层上不是“效果差”，而是数值上已经塌掉了。已有方法通常把补偿写成对量化误差的线性回归，再通过激活协方差矩阵的逆或伪逆求解补偿参数；一旦输入激活存在强冗余、近低秩，尤其再叠加激活量化噪声后，求解会沿着塌陷方向爆炸，得到巨大参数范数和负的 R²。更糟的是，现有基于 R² 的门控会把这些层判成“不值得补偿”，于是恰恰丢掉了最有价值的补偿层。作者把问题从“病态矩阵需要正则化”进一步收紧为“真正触发失败的是秩亏方向上的数值塌陷”，这是全文最关键的诊断。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，这不是训练方法论文，但它对大模型部署和量化研究有两个直接启发。第一，闭式补偿类 PTQ 方法的上限，不只取决于回归形式，还取决于你是否正确识别并保留了可补偿子空间；很多过去被当成‘这层补不了’的现象，其实是求解器坏了。第二，视觉 Transformer 的 W4A4 比 LLM 常见的 weight-only 更容易把这个问题放大出来，因为激活也被压到 4 bit，误差更大、输入结构更脆弱。论文因此把“数值稳定性”从实现细节提升成了方法设计的一部分。若把视角放宽到 LLM，凡是依赖 Hessian / activation covariance 逆的 PTQ、低秩补偿、二阶近似方法，都可以借这篇的诊断重新检查失败层到底是不可补，还是被错误筛掉了。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是更细粒度的 layer-wise compensation。作者不再像一些先前方法那样按 block 共享一个补偿器，而是给 Transformer block 内四个线性层分别挂独立补偿分支。补偿目标是拟合该层在真实部署输入分布下的量化误差，因此求解顺序也不是一次性离线完成，而是逐层顺序补偿：补完上一层后立刻把补偿后的层放回模型，再采集下一层的真实输入，避免训练时输入和部署时输入不一致。第二层是数值稳定求解，也是论文的核心。作者没有继续在原闭式解上做 damping 或 ridge，而是直接对激活矩阵对应的伪逆做截断，只去掉已经塌陷的奇异方向，保留其余可补偿子空间。这个 truncated pseudoinverse 是无额外超参或近乎参数自由的稳定化方案，目标不是缩小所有方向，而是只切掉那些会把解推向爆炸的方向。论文强调这和常规正则化不同：正则化会整体收缩，可能连有用方向一起伤到；截断伪逆则更像在做子空间清洗。第三层是部署压缩。补偿矩阵再做低秩分解，并对补偿参数继续量化，形成双轴压缩，在精度和额外存储之间找中间点。作者还专门研究了预算分配：不是按细粒度位置或层重要性去精打细算，而是按全局规模控制总 rank 更有效，因为‘重要层’并不等于‘对高 rank 更敏感的层’。","experiments":"结果里最有信息量的是几个反直觉点。1）在 ViT-B 的 W4A4 设置下，作者指出一个被旧门控最自信丢弃的层 b0.fc2，拟合时出现 R² = -68.6、补偿参数范数 6.2×10^7，看起来像完全不可用；但它其实是全模型最有价值的补偿层，去掉它会损失 1.05% top-1，超过任何其他单层。这个例子很直接地证明了“负 R² 不是低价值，而是数值塌陷报警”。2）方法在不额外训练的前提下，把 ViT-B/W4A4 做到 81.42% top-1，超过此前 training-free 方法，文中还称超过了基于微调的 QwT*。3）若进一步做低秩分解和补偿量化，得到一个更可部署的点：80.26% top-1，模型大小 54.7MB，精度和体积都落在 QwT 与 QwT-v2 之间，填上了此前“要么精度高但太重，要么很轻但补偿弱”的空档。4）独立层补偿相对 block-wise 补偿带来 +3.1% 的准确率增益，说明误差结构在 block 内并不适合共享一个补偿器。5）预算分配实验也很有意思：把‘高 rank 给重要层’的分配完全反过来，准确率只变 0.02%，说明层重要性和 rank 敏感性几乎解耦，这对后续做自动预算分配很重要。全文还强调这一失败机制同时出现在 CNN 和 Transformer 上，不是单一架构偶然现象。","reading_guide":"如果只花一小时，先看引言里关于负 R² 和被误删层的案例，再看方法部分对“rank deficiency 而非一般 ill-conditioning” 的区分，这是理解全文的钥匙。随后重点看三处：1）顺序式 layer-wise compensation 的求解流程，弄清为什么必须在每层补完后重新采样下游输入；2）截断伪逆的构造方式，以及它和 ridge / dampening 的差别，尤其是它保留可补偿子空间、只删除塌陷方向这一点；3）预算分配实验，作者为什么得出‘全局 rank 预算比细粒度分配更重要’。实验部分建议优先看 ViT-B/W4A4 主表、单层移除分析、block-wise vs layer-wise 消融、以及压缩后的精度-体积折中表。若想深挖，可以继续追两个问题：一是截断阈值是否真的跨模型稳定，二是这套诊断能否迁移到 LLM 的 weight-only / W8A8 PTQ 补偿里，尤其是 GPTQ、QERA、CALDERA 这类依赖二阶或协方差结构的方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in training-free W4A4 post-training quantization that is usually misread as “this layer is not worth compensating.” Closed-form residual compensation fits quantization error as a linear function of layer input and solves it through an inverse or pseudoinverse of the activation covariance. When activations are highly redundant or effectively low-rank, and activation quantization further perturbs them, the solver can collapse along degenerate directions. The result is exploding compensation norms, negative R² even on the fitted data, and a gate that incorrectly discards exactly the layers that would have contributed the most. The key diagnosis is sharper than the usual “ill-conditioned matrix” story: the real trigger is rank deficiency and numerical collapse in specific subspaces, not generic conditioning alone.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it matters for foundation-model deployment and for how we interpret PTQ failures. First, it shows that the ceiling of closed-form compensation methods depends as much on identifying the compensable subspace as on the regression form itself. Some layers previously treated as inherently uncompensable may simply have been filtered out by a broken solver. Second, the issue is amplified in W4A4, especially for vision Transformers, because activation quantization makes the regression target noisier and the input geometry more fragile than in weight-only settings common in LLM quantization. The broader lesson extends to LLM PTQ methods that rely on Hessian or activation-covariance inverses: before concluding that a layer cannot be repaired, one should check whether the optimization collapsed numerically rather than semantically.","technical_approach":"$11","experiments":"$12","reading_guide":"$13","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"建议看数值病态分析而不是只看指标；W4A4 失败常被归因于量化噪声，这篇把锅拆到 solver 稳定性。","en":"Read the numerical-pathology argument, not just the metrics; it shifts part of W4A4 failure from quantization noise to solver instability."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Lin-Fa Lee","Yi-Yu Chang","Kuo-Hei Yeh"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["post-training-quantization","W4A4","residual-compensation","numerical-stability","inference-efficiency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14149","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.14149","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.13756","title":"The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference","abstract":"Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T20:34:36Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13756.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在INT8量化LLM推理中，两个实现同一scaled linear kernel，其他全部固定。关键方法是利用整数累加的精确性作为“alibi”：在验证点积不溢出INT32的前提下，INT32累加结果与归约顺序无关，因此不可能是分歧来源。分歧只能发生在累加之后的scale应用、反量化和bfloat16/float32输出舍入阶段。作者随后做了逐层注入相同量化操作数的测量，用fp64仿真精确INT32累加来预测哪些位置应当bitwise一致；再通过一个干预实验验证定位：把scale舍入为2的幂，使scale乘法与舍入在满足条件时可交换，从而恢复bitwise相等。此外，作者用teacher-forced the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface—vLLM’s CUTLASS and Triton INT8 linear kernels—are truly interchangeable under fixed checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding strategy, and quantization configuration, and where in the compute graph any divergence actually arises.","importance":"$14","technical_approach":"The authors hold everything fixed and swap only the INT8 linear kernel inside vLLM. The key methodological device is the integer alibi: if no dot product overflows INT32, the INT32 accumulator is exact and independent of reduction order, so it cannot be the source of disagreement. Any cross-kernel difference must therefore arise after accumulation, in scale multiplication, dequantization, and output rounding. The authors feed both kernels identical quantized operands at every linear layer, emulate the exact INT32 accumulator in fp64 to predict where bitwise agreement must hold, and then validate the localization with an intervention: rounding scales to powers of two makes the scale multiplication commute with rounding under stated conditions and restores bitwise equality. They further separate single-step flips from autoregressive cascades using teacher-forced replay, predict flips from logit margin, and contrast INT8 exact-accumulator behavior against FP8 float-accumulator divergence across reduction depth.","experiments":"On Qwen3-1.7B, end-to-end greedy generation produced no agreeing sequences between CUTLASS and Triton in 0/8, 0/16, and 0/64 prompt comparisons, while each arm reproduced itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts. Per-layer exactness predictions held 196/196 and 252/252 on the 1.7B model. With real scales, cross-kernel differences never exceeded one bfloat16 spacing across 448 evaluated layers; switching to power-of-two scales removed divergence both layerwise and end-to-end. In teacher-forced measurements, logit margin predicted single-step token flips with ROC-AUC 0.942. In the FP8 contrast, exact-accumulator INT8 stayed at parts-per-million levels with no detectable trend in one unreplicated sweep, while float-accumulator FP8 showed percent-level divergence growing in both prevalence and magnitude with reduction depth, consistent with the authors’ saturation model. The paper explicitly states that power-of-two scales are not presented as a validated deployment mitigation.","reading_guide":"Skim. In this pap","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T07:30:36Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14038.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"文本到图像扩散/流匹配模型中，classifier-free guidance（CFG）通常使用固定全局guidance weight，忽略不同时间步、条件和当前噪声样本对引导强度的不同需求；论文研究如何自动学习依赖时间、条件和样本状态的CFG调度，而不是手工设计常量或时变表。","importance":"这是一篇生成模型采样方法论文，核心对象是图像diffusion/flow matching的CFG，不是LLM基座预训练本身。对预训练研究的直接启发有限，但有两点值得留意：其一，它把“训练时给出条件/无条件模型，采样时如何动态混合两个预测”转化为密度比估计和对抗训练问题，这一思路对离散扩散LM、masked LM或统一多模态模型中的guided decoding有概念参考价值；其二，它延续了最近把采样策略从静态超参数改为自适应、可学习策略的趋势，这和语言模型中自适应temperature、长度惩罚、verifier-guided decoding属于同一类问题。但总体上它更偏图像生成推理控制，不属于必须跟踪的预训练主线。","technical_approach":"论文以flow matching为主要形式化对象，把guidance schedule建模为时间t、条件c和当前噪声样本x_t的函数，而不是全局常数。理论约束从之前工作中较强的self-consistency退回到较弱的marginal consistency：希望guided采样轨迹在每个时间步的边际分布尽量匹配真实数据加噪后的边际分布。实现上不用MMD或能量距离，而是采用对抗框架：训练一个判别器估计真实样本与CFG引导样本之间随时间变化的log-density ratio，再优化guidance调度器，使引导轨迹在各时间步的边际更接近真实边际。这样得到的guidance weight可以同时依赖时间、prompt和当前latent state，相比常数CFG和手工时变schedule更细粒度。","experiments":"实验在标准T2I基准上对比常数CFG、手工时变CFG以及先前的learned guidance基线。论文报告在样本质量（FID）和文本对齐指标上同时优于这些基线，说明只要求marginal consistency的对抗式密度比学习，比之前追求self-consistency但对齐受限的方法更有效。论文没有把方法改成生成模型自身的训练目标，也没有讨论对大模型scaling的影响；主要收益集中在采样阶段的调度自适应。消融重点显示时间、条件和样本依赖三者都有贡献，常数或纯时间依赖的schedule不足以同时兼顾保真度和prompt adherence。","reading_guide":"泛读即可。重点看引言和方法部分如何从marginal consistency推到density ratio/GAN形式，以及实验部分与静态CFG、手工schedule、Galashov等先前learned schedule的对比。若不做图像diffusion，不必细抠flow matching推导。预计阅读时间20–30分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Classifier-free guidance in text-to-image diffusion and flow-matching models typically uses a fixed global guidance weight, even though different timesteps, prompts, and noisy states likely require different amounts of guidance. The paper studies how to learn a CFG schedule that depends on time, conditioning, and the current noisy sample rather than relying on hand-tuned constants or hand-designed time-varying schedules.","importance":"This is a sampling-methods paper for image diffusion/flow matching rather than a core LLM pretraining paper. Its direct relevance to foundation-model pretraining is limited, but two ideas are worth noting. First, it reframes the sampling-time mixture of conditional and unconditional predictions as a density-ratio estimation and adversarial learning problem, which is conceptually relevant to guided decoding in discrete diffusion LMs, masked LMs, or unified multimodal models. Second, it continues the broader trend of turning static sampling hyperparameters into adaptive, learned policies, parallel to adaptive temperature, length penalties, or verifier-guided decoding in language models. Even so, the paper is primarily about image-generation inference control and is not central to the pretraining research stack.","technical_approach":"The paper is formalized mainly in flow matching and models the guidance schedule as a function of timestep t, conditioning c, and current noisy state x_t, instead of a global scalar. It weakens the requirement from the stronger self-consistency objective used in prior learned-guidance work to a marginal-consistency condition: the guided sampling trajectory should match the true noised-data marginals at each timestep. Rather than using MMD or energy-distance kernels, the authors adopt an adversarial framework: a discriminator is trained to estimate the time-dependent log-density ratio between real samples and classifier-free guided samples, while the guidance scheduler is optimized to make guided marginals closer to the true marginals at all timesteps. The resulting guidance weight is therefore adaptive to time, prompt, and latent state, making it more expressive than constant CFG or manually designed schedules.","experiments":"On standard text-to-image benchmarks, the method is compared against constant CFG, hand-designed dynamic CFG schedules, and prior learned-guidance baselines. The paper reports improvements in both sample quality, measured by FID, and text-alignment metrics, indicating that adversarial density-ratio learning under the weaker marginal-consistency objective can outperform earlier self-consistency-based methods that improved distribution match but lagged in prompt adherence. The method is a sampling-time improvement rather than a change to generative-model training, and the paper does not study scaling effects on large models. Ablations suggest that time, conditioning, and sample dependence all contribute; constant or purely time-dependent schedules are insufficient to balance fidelity and prompt adherence.","reading_guide":"Skim. Focus on the introduction and method sections for the move from marginal consistency to a density-ratio/GAN objective, and on the experimental comparison with static CFG, hand-tuned schedules, and prior learned schedules such as Galashov et al. Readers outside image diffusion can skip much of the flow-matching derivation. 闭环都很关键。第三，它提出的显式 theory-of-mind 监督不是停留在“让模型解释自己”，而是把 Infer→Act→Anticipate 作为可蒸馏的中间结构，并分析哪类推理信号真正和效用提升相关。全文里一个很有价值的判断是：next-action prediction 比较稳定地预测谈判结果，这提示 social reasoning 里最有用的不是泛泛的心理描写，而是对对手下一步行为的可操作预测。","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"实验设计比较扎实，重点有四类。第一类是单环境 specialist 训练。作者在六个 held-out scenario 上评估 4B specialist，与更大的 GPT 模型比较。摘要里给出的结论是，domain-trained 4B policies 在多个环境上达到与 much larger GPT models 可比的效用，这说明 targeted post-training 的收益足以部分替代参数规模。第二类是跨环境迁移。论文没有把迁移当作附带结果，而是显式构造了 full transfer matrix。结果显示迁移是“有结构的”：交互形式相近的环境之间迁移最强，例如价格谈判类任务之间、或偏好协调类任务之间更容易互相受益。这一点很重要，因为它说明模型学到的不是环境表面词汇，而是某种 interaction structure。\n\n第三类是统一模型整合。通过 cascade RL 和 MOPD，把多个 specialist 合并成一个 4B unified policy。关键数字是：统一 4B 模型在六环境上的平均 utility 达到 0.627，而文中对比的 GPT-4.1 为 0.625，GPT-5.1 为 0.619，GPT-5.2 为 0.613。这个结果的含义不是“小模型全面超过大模型”，而是说明在明确定义的 delegated social reasoning 任务上，训练目标和训练信号的匹配程度可以比通用能力更重要。\n\n第四类是 theory-of-mind 蒸馏与分析。Infer→Act→Anticipate 的蒸馏提升了性能和跨环境泛化。更细的分析指出，next-action prediction 是最能预测 negotiation outcome 的 ToM 子技能。也就是说，如果只能保留一种中间监督，优先保留对对手下一步动作的预测，而不是更宽泛的偏好描述或自我解释。摘要没有给出所有消融数字，但从全文叙述看，作者做了至少以下几类消融：是否使用显式 ToM 轨迹、不同 consolidation 方法的比较、以及 specialist 与 unified policy 的差异。若精读，建议重点核对 transfer matrix、ToM 子任务相关性分析、以及 unified model 相对各 specialist 的损失位置。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题定义→系统→训练→迁移→ToM 分析”的顺序读。先看 Introduction 和任务定义部分，抓住一个核心判断：assistant alignment 与 strategic delegation 的目标并不一致。这个判断决定了后面所有设计。然后看环境与基础设施章节，理解为什么作者坚持 decoupled system；如果你关心 multi-turn RL 或 agent training infra，这一部分比算法细节更有复用价值。\n\n接着重点看 specialist 训练和 cross-environment transfer matrix。这里能回答两个关键问题：SocialRL 到底是在学环境模板，还是在学更抽象的交互策略；以及哪些环境之间共享结构。再看 unified policy 的 consolidation 部分，尤其是 cascade RL 与 MOPD 的差异。若你做 continual post-training、多任务 RL 或 teacher aggregation，这部分值得细抠。\n\n最后精读 Infer→Act→Anticipate 相关章节和对应图表。重点不是看模型会不会“解释”，而是看哪类中间监督真正带来效用提升。建议特别关注 next-action prediction 与最终 utility 的相关性分析，以及作者如何构造和蒸馏这些 reasoning traces。整篇如果只花 45 分钟，优先看：任务定义、六环境设置、transfer matrix、统一模型主结果表、ToM 消融。若要深挖 2 小时以上，就把训练基础设施和 consolidation recipe 一并读透。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a failure mode that standard assistant training does not solve: when a model acts as a delegate for a user in adversarial or partially conflicting interactions, being polite, transparent, and eager to agree can directly hurt the principal. The core problem is whether a small model can be post-trained to perform principal-conditioned social reasoning: infer the counterpart’s incentives, protect private information, decide when to concede or resist, and optimize the user’s utility rather than merely keeping the conversation smooth. The paper argues that current assistant-style alignment objectives systematically bias models toward cooperative dialogue behavior, which becomes a liability in negotiation, bargaining, and coordination settings.","importance":"$1a","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"$1d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 RL 目标和 cross-domain 表格；小模型追平大模型的结论有价值，但要先确认对手设置是否公平。","en":"Read the RL objective and cross-domain table; the small-model-beats-frontier claim is useful only if the opponent setup is clean."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research","Microsoft AI Frontiers","Affiliation:Microsoft Research, AI Frontiers"],"authors":["Wenyue Hua","Zachary Huang","Tyler Payne","Safoora Yousefi","Saleema Amershi","Asli Celikyilmaz"],"notable_authors":["Saleema Amershi","Asli Celikyilmaz"],"tags":["RLHF","social-reasoning","agent-training","negotiation","small-models"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13787","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.13787","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.13698","title":"GRPO Beyond English: A Large-Scale Study of GRPO in Non-English and Multilingual Settings","abstract":"Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), often optimized with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has become a central recipe for improving the reasoning capabilities of pretrained language models but current studies remain heavily English-centric. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of multilingual and non-English GRPO across a wide range of base models, training languages, and different reasoning language rewards. We find that training to reason in the native language","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T18:43:33Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13698.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“GRPO 在多语言上能不能用”，而是更细的三个问题：第一，RLVR/GRPO 在非英语训练语言下是否仍然有效；第二，奖励模型要求用英语推理还是要求用题目原生语言推理，会怎样改变最终能力；第三，单语 GRPO 会不会向其他语言迁移，或者反过来带来跨语言退化。作者把问题放在一个较大规模、强控制变量的设定里做：9 个基座模型，11 个训练语言，单语与多语两种训练方式，并区分 English-reasoning reward 与 native-reasoning reward。全文最重要的结论是，多语言 RL 后训练的行为远比英文场景更依赖“模型家族 × 语言 × 奖励语言”的组合，不能从单一模型或单一语言外推。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它直接补上了 RL post-train 与 multilingual pretrain 之间长期缺失的接口分析：很多模型明明做了多语言预训练，但 RL 阶段几乎只在英语上优化，这篇工作说明这种做法并不天然最优，至少在一部分语言和模型上，原生语言推理奖励只比英语推理奖励略差，甚至能带来更好的语言忠实度。第二，它揭示了一个更值得警惕的现象：GRPO 的收益和副作用都具有明显语言选择性，某些语言训练会给未见语言或其他任务带来显著回退。这对后续做 multilingual continual post-training、reasoning language control、以及预训练阶段的语言配比设计都有直接启发。贡献类型以系统性经验研究为主，不是新算法，但实验覆盖面和结论密度足够支撑后续 recipe 设计。","technical_approach":"方法上没有引入新的 RL 算法，核心价值在实验设计。数据使用 Multilingual Reasoning Gym，可程序化生成 14 种语言的推理样本，并带有可调难度课程。作者没有直接把英文题翻译后拿来训，而是利用在线难度调节：某任务最近 32 次 rollout 正确率超过 70% 就升难度，低于 10% 就降难度。这样做是因为同一模型在不同语言上的能力基线不同，固定难度会把语言差异和训练效果混在一起。为了避免小模型在过难任务上完全拿不到 reward，作者先用 Qwen3-14B-Base 在默认简单难度上做筛选，去掉 pass@8 为 0 的任务；同时去掉训练集中英语内容过重的任务，只保留作评测。最终主训练集 62 个任务，OOD 评测集 30 个未见任务。\n\n训练上，作者统一采用 DAPO 风格的 GRPO loss，不加 KL penalty。每步 64 个 prompt、每个 prompt 8 个 rollout，共 500 step，学习率 1e-6。奖励由三部分组成：二值 correctness reward 为主；只有答案正确时，额外给 format reward 和 reasoning-language reward，各自权重是 correctness 的一半。reasoning-language reward 分成两种设定：要求模型用英语写推理，或要求模型用 prompt 对应语言写推理。这里的一个细节很关键：语言奖励只在答案正确时发放，避免模型为了刷语言/格式分而牺牲解题正确率，作者也提到早期实验里低资源语言容易出现过拟合格式奖励甚至训练塌陷。\n\n实验矩阵覆盖 Qwen、Gemma、SmolLM 三个家族，既有 base 也有 instruction/post-trained 版本，参数规模从 1B 到 8B。作者对 11 种 MGSM 语言都做了单语训练，并做了一个均匀采样语言的多语训练；意大利语、韩语、葡萄牙语完全留作 held-out language，用来测跨语言泛化。对于具备足够目标语言初始支持的模型-语言组合，再额外训练 native-reward 版本。整个设计的重点不是追求单点最好成绩，而是把“训练语言”“推理语言奖励”“模型预训练背景”这几个因素拆开看。","experiments":"从全文给出的结果看，最稳定的结论有四个。第一，native-reasoning reward 平均只比 English-reasoning reward 有小幅差距，不存在普遍性的巨大性能损失。这说明在很多模型上，要求模型用本地语言思考并不会像早期工作担心的那样显著伤害最终正确率。第二，跨语言迁移很强：在一种语言上做 GRPO，往往会提升多种其他语言上的表现，说明 RL 学到的不只是表层语言模板，也包括可迁移的解题策略。第三，这种迁移并不单调，存在明显的 regression failure mode：某些模型在某种语言上训练后，会让其他未训练语言或某些评测集显著下降。作者反复强调，这种现象高度依赖具体模型和语言，不能简单归因为“低资源语言更差”或“英语总是最好”。第四，多语训练并不总是优于最佳单语训练，但通常能提供更稳健的折中，尤其在 held-out language 上更有意义。\n\n实验评测覆盖 seen tasks、30 个未见任务、MGSM 和 PolyMath。摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但全文明确说明结论来自 9 个模型、11 个训练语言、单语/多语、English/native reward 的大规模对照。对预训练研究者更有价值的是消融逻辑：作者不是只看平均分，而是看不同语言训练对其他语言的转移矩阵，以及不同模型家族在同一语言上的反应差异。这个分析支持一个重要判断：多语言 RL 的主要瓶颈不是“有没有多语言数据”，而是模型在各语言上的初始能力、reward 可得性、以及训练后策略漂移之间的耦合。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和实验设置，重点理解作者为什么坚持用可调难度的程序化数据，而不是直接翻译英文数学集。然后看结果部分里按训练语言、奖励语言、模型家族拆开的主表和迁移分析图，尤其留意那些“某语言训练提升本语言但伤害其他语言”的案例。Appendix 里任务筛选、held-out task 构造、以及哪些模型-语言组合具备 native reasoning 初始支持，也值得扫一遍，因为这些细节决定了结论边界。预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主结论；如果你关心 multilingual RL recipe，建议额外花时间看 failure cases 和 reward 设计说明。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a more precise question than “does GRPO work beyond English?” It studies three coupled issues: whether RLVR/GRPO remains effective when training is done in non-English languages, whether rewarding English reasoning versus native-language reasoning changes the outcome, and whether monolingual GRPO transfers across languages or induces regressions elsewhere. The key contribution is not a new algorithm but a controlled, large experimental matrix over 9 base models, 11 training languages, monolingual and multilingual training, and two reasoning-language reward settings. The main takeaway is that multilingual RL behavior is highly dependent on the specific model-family × language × reward-language combination, so conclusions from a single English-centric setup do not generalize well.","importance":"Its value for pretraining-oriented research is in the interface it exposes between multilingual pretraining and RL post-training. Many open models are multilingual at pretrain time, yet their reasoning RL stage is still almost entirely English. This study shows that such a recipe is not automatically optimal: rewarding native-language reasoning often leaves only a small gap relative to English-reasoning rewards, while potentially improving language faithfulness. More importantly, the paper documents language-specific regression modes, where GRPO in one language can hurt unseen languages or other tasks. That matters for multilingual continual post-training, reasoning-language control, and even pretraining decisions such as language mixture and capability balancing. The contribution is empirical rather than algorithmic, but the scale and control make it useful for recipe design.","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and setup sections to understand why the paper insists on procedurally generated, difficulty-adaptive data instead of translated English math sets. Then read the main results broken down by training language, rewarded reasoning language, and model family. Pay special attention to the transfer analyses and the cases where one language improves itself but harms others. The appendix is worth skimming for task filtering, held-out task construction, and the criteria for deciding whether a model-language pair has enough initial support for native-language reasoning. Around 25–35 minutes is enough to extract the main lessons; spend more time on the failure cases if you care about multilingual RL recipes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不在算法上出新，但会直接影响多语RL配方：先看语言迁移和能力回退那几张主表，再决定内部GRPO是否继续英语中心化。","en":"No new RL algorithm here, but it can change your multilingual RL recipe; read the main transfer/regression tables before defaulting to English-centric GRPO."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Apple","Affiliation:Apple"],"authors":["Konstantin Dobler","Federico Scozzafava","Jonathan Janke","Mohamed Ali","Simon Lehnerer"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["GRPO","RLVR","multilingual","cross-lingual-transfer","reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13698","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.13698","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.14277","title":"SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning","abstract":"On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T12:57:02Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14277.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但现实的问题：如何把长上下文强推理教师模型的能力，用 on-policy distillation 迁移到上下文更短、甚至 tokenizer 完全不同的学生模型上。已有 OPD 多建立在同家族、同词表设定上，而一旦教师和学生来自不同模型家族，就会同时遇到四个问题：token 级对齐失效、teacher-student 分布差距大、教师偏好的长推理轨迹超出学生上下文预算、以及训练中终止 token 被持续压制导致响应长度失控。作者的目标不是提出复杂框架，而是给出一个足够简单、能跨 tokenizer 稳定工作的 OPD recipe。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口研究的意义在于，它把“强教师推理能力如何迁移”这件事从同构蒸馏推进到了异构蒸馏。很多高能力教师模型依赖更长上下文、更重的推理模板或不同 tokenizer，直接做 SFT 往往只能学到静态轨迹，直接做 OPD 又会因为长度爆炸和 token 不对齐而不稳定。SimpleOPD 说明，跨 tokenizer 的 on-policy 蒸馏并不一定需要显式构造复杂 token 映射，只要在共享文本空间里做可靠对齐，再加上对长度漂移的约束，就能把长推理教师的能力迁到短上下文学生上。对做 reasoning post-train、teacher-student recipe、以及未来 pretrain/post-train 一体化设计的人，这是一条很实用的路线。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。第一，跨 tokenizer 对齐不在 token id 空间做，而在共享文本空间做。学生先按自己的 chat template 和 tokenizer 生成响应 token 序列，再解码成表面字符串；教师端不直接吃学生 token，而是用自己的 chat template 重建上下文，把同一段学生响应文本拼进去，再用教师 tokenizer 重新编码。随后只在“学生 token 与教师 token 覆盖完全相同文本 span”的位置上做对齐和蒸馏。这样做避免了人为定义不同词表之间的 token 对应关系，也绕开了 chat template 不同带来的上下文格式差异。代价是并非所有 token 都能对齐，但作者认为只保留高置信对齐位置，比错误密集对齐更稳。\n\n第二，作者处理了 OPD 在长教师到短学生场景下最棘手的长度失控问题。直接 OPD 时，教师会在学生生成的每一步上给出偏好分布；如果教师本身偏好更长的思维链，学生就会不断被鼓励“继续想”，导致 think 段和最终回答越来越长，最终频繁截断。为此作者引入 student reference KL loss，把当前学生策略约束在初始学生策略附近，抑制过快策略漂移。文中明确指出，teacher-student gap 越大，KL 正则通常需要越强。这个设计和 RLHF/RLVR 里的 reference model 约束有相似动机，但这里服务的是蒸馏稳定性和长度控制。\n\n第三，作者对特殊终止 token 做 advantage masking。观察是：如果教师在很多位置上都认为“继续生成”比“现在结束”更优，那么直接把这种偏好传给学生，会系统性压制 </think>、<|im_end|> 之类的终止 token，训练很快进入无法正常收尾的状态。于是作者在这些特殊 token 上屏蔽 advantage，不让教师监督直接惩罚终止行为。这个改动很小，但针对的是长推理蒸馏里一个非常具体的病灶。\n\n整体上，SimpleOPD 不是重新发明 OPD，而是把跨 tokenizer 对齐、长度约束、终止稳定性三个工程上最容易出问题的点收拢成一个简单 recipe。作者还强调，他们没有用教师离线轨迹做 SFT 预热，主要依赖 on-policy 过程本身完成迁移。","experiments":"实验围绕从长上下文推理模型 SU-01 向多个短上下文学生蒸馏展开，学生覆盖 Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2-Preview、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4 等不同家族，既有同家族也有跨家族设定。最醒目的结果是 Intern-S2-Preview 在 ProofBench 上从 34.0 提升到 55.2，增幅 21.2 分，超过 系数通常要调得更强，否则学生会被教师拉向自己根本承载不了的长轨迹分布。\n\n从实验叙事看，这篇工作的重点不是追求单 benchmark 的最高分，而是证明一个简单 recipe 能在异构模型之间稳定工作，并解释为什么直接 OPD 会失败。对读者最有价值的不是单个数字，而是长度分布、截断率、终止 token 行为这些训练动态分析。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Method important problem: how to transfer reasoning ability from a long-context teacher into shorter-context student models using on-policy distillation, even when teacher and student use different tokenizers and different chat templates. Prior OPD work mostly assumes same-family models with shared vocabularies. Once that assumption is removed, four issues appear at once: token-level alignment breaks, teacher and student occupy very different trajectory distributions, the teacher prefers responses that exceed the student’s context budget, and termination tokens get systematically suppressed, causing runaway response length and unstable training. The goal of SimpleOPD is to provide a minimal recipe that makes this heterogeneous OPD setting work reliably.","importance":"Its significance lies in extending reasoning distillation from homogeneous to heterogeneous teacher-student settings. Many strong reasoning teachers rely on longer contexts, different tokenizers, and different prompting formats. Static SFT on teacher traces often loses the benefits of on-policy supervision, while naive OPD becomes unstable because of length explosion and token mismatch. This work shows that cross-tokenizer OPD does not require a complicated learned token mapping. If supervision is moved into a shared text space and policy drift is controlled, long-context reasoning behavior can be transferred into shorter students. That is practically useful for reasoning post-training, strong-to-weak transfer, and future designs that more tightly connect pretraining and post-training.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"For a quick pass, start with the method section that defines shared-text-space alignment and the token-span matching rule. context-appr","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T01:51:53Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13622.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是开放式真实交互中的 group-based RL 比较偏差。常规 GRPO/RLHF 一类方法会在同一 prompt 的多个 rollout 内做相对优势估计，但在 agent 交互里，同一个状态可能有多种合理策略：直接回答、先澄清、执行中汇报进度、关键动作前确认等。若把这些不同策略放在同一组里比较，reward model 对长度、语气、交互风格的偏好会混进优势估计，导致“策略偏好”被误当成“质量差异”。","importance":"它对预训练研究的直接贡献不在基座训练本身，而在 post-train 信号质量。对基座研究员有启发的一点是：相对奖励并不天然公平，比较集合的构造会改变梯度含义。这个问题在开放式交互、工具使用和长轨迹训练里会更明显。ARC 给出的策略条件化比较，类似把 rollout 按行为模式分层后再做组内归一化，能减少 reward 信号中由风格区域差异带来的噪声。INTER3 的数据格式也有参考价值，因为它把用户可见输出、潜在推理和工具执行拆开，使交互行为可以被标注和控制。","technical_approach":"方法由两部分组成。第一部分是 ARC，即 Advantage Regularization via Conditioning。训练时给每个样本附加一个策略 instruction，在同一策略条件下采样多个 rollout，只在这个策略条件组内计算相对 advantage，并配合 hybrid reward 与 entropy regularization 更新策略。推理时移除策略 instruction，让模型自行选择交互策略。这个设计不同于 hint-based RL：hint 不是用来降低任务难度或暴露解题结构，而是用来限定比较域，让相对奖励比较的是同一类行为里的质量差异。第二部分是 INTER3。它将普通文本作为 latent reasoning，将 `<answer>` span 作为流式展示给用户的内容，将 tool call 保持为结构化内部动作。这样可以显式建模进度更新、澄清、对齐确认、用户打断和中途转向。作者还构造了 RL 上优于普通 group-based RL。诊断实验的主要结论是：训练时加入策略 hint 有帮助，但推理时去掉 hint 得到更好的整体行为。这说明模型不是简单依赖 prompt 里的策略标签，而是通过更干净的组内比较学到了可泛化的交互策略。理论分析把标准组内中心化 advantage 是否学到通用策略的关键。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies an unfair-comparison problem in group-based RL for open-ended agent interaction. Standard relative-advantage methods compare multiple rollouts for the same prompt. In real interaction, however, several next actions can all be valid: answer directly, ask for clarification, report progress while tools run, or confirm before an irreversible step. If these strategies are compared in one group, reward-model preferences over length, tone, or interaction style can leak into the centered advantage and be mistaken for quality differences.","importance":"The main relevance is post-training signal quality rather than base-model pretraining. The useful lesson is that relative rewards are only as clean as the comparison set. In open-ended tool-use trajectories, the group construction can change the meaning of the gradient. ARC treats strategy as a conditioning variable and computes relative advantages within comparable behavioral regions. This is a practical way to reduce reward noise caused by cross-strategy variation. INTER3 is also useful as a data-interface idea: it separates user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool execution, making interaction strategies observable and annotatable.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"The reported concrete numbers include a TTFT reduction from 4.91s to 1.27s compared with a think-then-act baseline, and an 86K-example strategy-annotated corpus. Empirically, ARC improves open-ended tool-use RL when trained on INTER3-86K. The most informative diagnostic is that strategy hints help during training, while removing them at inference gives the best overall behavior. This suggests that the model is not merely following prompt-side strategy labels; the cleaner comparison groups help it learn a more general interaction policy. The theory section decomposes centered-advantage variance and identifies inter-strategy variance as an error source that ideal strategy conditioning removes.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Given multiple teacher-generated solutions for the same problem, should one train on the response that is already high-likelihood under the student, or on a low-likelihood response that is harder for the student to imitate? The main finding is capacity dependence. High-likelihood responses fit quickly. Low-likelihood responses can be harmful or unstable for small students, but can produce larger gains for sufficiently capable models when training is long enough.","importance":"The result is useful for the interface between synthetic reasoning data and post-training. Many reasoning models are trained from strong-teacher traces, and response selection is often treated as a fixed rule. This paper shows that the rule should depend on student capacity and compute budget. Easy, high-likelihood data gives stable early improvement; harder, low-likelihood data can push a larger model beyond its current distribution. This has direct implications for curriculum design, data filtering, and difficulty scheduling during SFT or continual training.","technical_approach":"The setup is controlled and simple. The authors use MATH12K as the instruction pool and generate candidate answers with two teachers, Qwen2.5-72B and gemma-3-27b-it. For each candidate response, they compute conditional log-likelihood under the base student model. They then construct high-likelihood and low-likelihood datasets and train students with standard SFT. The student scales include Qwen2.5-1.5B, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. Evaluation uses pass@1 with temperature 0.6 across ten math benchmarks. The contribution is not a new optimizer or objective; it is a careful study of how likelihood-based selection interacts with model capacity and training duration.","experiments":"The provided text reports experiments on MATH12K with two teacher models and students from 1.5B to 8B parameters, evaluated on ten math datasets including AIME24, AMC, and CHMATH. The key empirical pattern is Fast-Fit / Slow-Gain. High-likelihood data improves models faster at the beginning. Low-likelihood data learns more slowly, but for larger students and longer training can lead to stronger final reasoning performance. For smaller students, low-likelihood data often fails to move the model toward the teacher distribution and can produce repetitive or shallow outputs. The authors also report similar trends for other model families in an appendix.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus first on Figure 1 and the main empirical curves, since the claim depends on learning dynamics rather than a single final score. Then read the response-selection formulation to verify that likelihood is computed under the base student model and is not updated online. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. Using self-consistency as a represent","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T16:01:10Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14420.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Test-time scaling场景下，Power Sampling作为无验证器的轨迹分布锐化方法，被广泛认为可以提升多轨迹推理（如自一致性Self-Consistency）的效果，但实际使用中会出现「正确轨迹的总概率质量提升，最终聚合答案准确率反而下降」的悖论，现有研究未解释该现象根因，也未提出适配多轨迹聚合目标的修复方案。","importance":"该工作指出了test-time compute方向一个容易被忽略的分布对齐问题：轨迹级别的分布优化和答案级别的聚合决策目标并不天然一致，纠正了「只要提升正确轨迹概率就一定提升多采样推理效果」的认知偏差；提出的修复方法不需要额外训练或外部验证器，可直接复用现有Power Sampling的并行采样框架，对test-time scaling的实际落地有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"该工作首先定位悖论的两个根因：1）覆盖失配：Power Sampling按单条轨迹概率做指数加权，会放大单条高概率错误轨迹的权重，压制多个中等概率、指向同一正确答案的轨迹的聚合支持，最终让错误答案占优；2）剂量失配：固定的锐化指数α在不同prompt上造成的分布偏移程度差异极大，部分问题上会导致分布几乎坍缩到单条轨迹。针对两个问题，该工作提出Relative-Rank SoftSat修复方案：1）用相对排名代替原始似然作为加权依据，把每个prompt内的轨迹排名归一化到[0,1]区间，让不同问题上的锐化强度可比；2）SoftSat加权函数对高排名轨迹的增益做饱和处理，提升中等排名轨迹的相对权重，避免少数头部轨迹吞噬绝大多数概率质量；同时提出同预算的重要性加权聚合实现，不需要为目标分布单独采样，直接复用基础模型采样的N条候选轨迹做加权聚合，和原始Self-Consistency的计算开销一致。","experiments":"该工作在9个模型-基准测试组合上验证，原始固定指数Power Sampling在7个组合上导致下游准确率下降，最大降幅达18.5个百分点，在LiveAoPSBench、PHYSICS等推理基准上全部出现性能退化；修复后的Relative-Rank SoftSat在相同计算预算下避免了性能退化问题，同时保留了Power Sampling锐化分布的收益，多轨迹聚合的准确率稳定超过基础模型+Self-Consistency的基线。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点看引言部分的悖论示例和两个失配的成因解释，图1的四个子图清晰呈现了问题和方案的核心逻辑；方案推导和实现部分可以快速浏览，核心收获是理解「轨迹级优化不等于答案级优化」这一观察，预计阅读时间20分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Power Sampling, a verifier-free method that sharpens LLM trajectory distributions for test-time reasoning, is widely assumed to improve multi-trajectory aggregation methods such as Self-Consistency, but exhibits an underexplored paradox: it can increase total probability mass on correct trajectories while reducing final answer accuracy. every","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T20:27:56Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13754.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要回答的问题很直接：机制可解释性里常见的 有没有信息量，而是在问它是否具有可复核性。这个问题对可解释性研究很关键，因为如果解释结果对分析规格高度敏感，那么它更像一种研究性线索，而不是可审计证据。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响有限，它不是训练方法、数据或建模范式论文。但它对机制分析和 interpretability 的方法论有明确提醒：很多 circuit-level 结论可能没有想象中稳，尤其当研究者在 机制分析的人，这篇文章的价值在于把“分析者自由度”量化了，并给出一个接近审计场景的稳定性标准。它不告诉你怎么训练更好的模型，但会影响你如何看待解释性证据的可信度。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者做的是一个预注册的 multiverse analysis。核心思想不是挑一套分析配置跑实验，而是系统枚举一组都能自圆其说、且都来自已发表实现的分析规格，然后观察结论在这些规格之间如何变化。\n\n具体来说，一个 specification 由多个分析轴组成，包括 discovery objective、ablation operator、corruption distribution、evaluation metric、size threshold、prompt variant、seed、granularity。每个轴的取值都来自已有文献实现，而不是作者临时发明。研究对象是 GPT-2 small 上的 indirect object identification 任务，使用 factorized graph 形式的 circuit discovery。对每个 specification，方法返回一个电路子图 C(s)。\n\n关键设计在于作者没有直接比较“电路长得像不像”，而是引入一个确定性的 claim map φ，把发现的 circuit 映射成结构化的 Annex IV 风格陈述。这样评估对象从“图结构差异”变成“监管语境下的解释陈述差异”。作者定义了 flip rate、modal share π*、以及条件化后的 within-group 版本，并提出一个 filability criterion：只有当 modal share 满足 π* ≥ 1 − α 时，解释证据才算达到某个容忍度下的可提交标准。\n\n论文还特别强调一个统计问题：当不同 specification 选出的 circuit 大小不同，简单 pooled 统计可能发生反转，因此他们同时报告 pooled、固定大小、去除 size 影响等多种分析。这个部分的方法论意义比具体任务本身更强，因为它指出 multiverse 设计里对象大小变化会污染稳定性结论。","experiments":"实验规模相当大：作者在 GPT-2 small 和 IOI 任务上运行了 15,840 个预注册 specification，其中 7,561 个产生了可映射的 claim。主要结果有几条。\n\n第一，解释陈述的变动很大。论文报告的 flip rate 为 0.7316；在观察到 9 个 claim class 的情况下，这已经达到理论上限 0.8889 的 82.3%。换句话说，从两个合理 specification 随机抽样，得到不同监管陈述的概率很高。\n\n第二，modal share 很低，意味着很难满足作者提出的 filability criterion。也就是说，如果把 circuit discovery 结果当成正式技术文档的一部分，当前方法在这个标准下不够稳。\n\n第三，这种不稳定性不是由随机种子主导，也不是简单因为电路大小不同。作者做了多种 aggressive control：固定单一分析轴、从 claim map 中去掉 circuit size、以及在固定 circuit size 条件下比较，结论仍然成立。\n\n第四，电路并非毫无信息。作者明确给出对照：在固定 circuit size 时，真实发现的 circuit 稳定性优于 size-matched random null，数值为 0.2746 对 0.4230；但功能不稳定性依然明显，pooled 为 0.3972，在最小电路时达到 0.4777。另一个很强的信号是 Cohen’s kappa 只有 0.0146，说明不同 specification 找到的 circuit 在功能上几乎不相关。\n\n这些结果共同支持一个较克制的结论：circuit discovery 不是随机噪声，但它目前还不足以支撑高一致性的可审计解释。","reading_guide":"这篇文章不适合只看摘要。建议先看 Introduction 和 “What this paper is not claiming”，因为作者对结论边界写得很清楚，能避免误读成“机制解释没用”。\n\n然后看 A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-14T17:47:13Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_14528.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","econ.EM","math.ST","stat.ME","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当LLM应用因上下文长度限制、应用重启或跨智能体协作需要开启新会话时，如何确定旧会话中哪些信息必须传递，才能保证新会话的预测与决策质量与完整上下文下一致，并量化所需的存储开销。","importance":"会话交接的理论框架将上下文学习视为可编码的状态，为分析ICL的信息保留机制提供了形式化工具，也为长上下文模型设计、上下文压缩算法、智能体系统记忆模块的构建提供了可量化的理论基准。","technical_approach":"将会话交接形式化为「查询前状态编码问题」，与已知查询的提示压缩有本质区别。框架分离四个独立组件：生成交接记录的writer、交接记录本身、续接流程、任务得分。引入统计充分性、贝叶斯决策论、信源编码等工具：在外生性条件下，预测等价性刻画了最粗粒度的确定性充分交接，给出固定长度比特需求；将性能损失分解为记忆约束导致的容量损失、writer选择导致的损失、以及续接流程与理想解码器的差距；在对数损失下，丢弃的任务信息严格等价于条件互信息。论文还在高斯线性回归和非参数回归两个设定下，推导了具体的充分记录构造和记忆-风险权衡关系。","experiments":"这是一篇理论性工作，无大规模实证实验。核心结果通过解析推导得到：高斯线性回归设定下存在精确的有限维充分记录，可等价表示为合成的充分示例，并能给出后验预测的有限比特扰动界；非参数回归设定下给出了可达到的记忆-风险关系，以及平方损失任务的样本量和记忆下界。","reading_guide":"泛读先看引言的问题定义和四个核心贡献，再看会话交接概述章节的核心概念区分（交接记录与KV缓存、外部记录的区别，交接与提示压缩的区别）。重点理解预测充分性框架和损失分解的三层结构。预计阅读时间25-35分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"When an LLM application needs to start a new session due to context length limits, application restarts, or cross-agent handoff, how to determine which information from the previous session must be passed on to preserve prediction and decision quality matching the full context, and how to quantify the required storage overhead.","importance":"The theoretical framework of session handover treats in-context learning as an encodable state, providing formal tools to analyze information retention in ICL. It also offers quantifiable theoretical baselines for long-context model design, context compression algorithms, and memory module construction in agent systems.","technical_approach":"Session handover is formulated as a pre-query state coding problem, fundamentally distinct from query-aware prompt compression. The framework separates four independent components: the writer that produces the handover record, the record itself, the continuation procedure, and the task score. Using tools from statistical sufficiency, Bayesian decision theory, and source coding: under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. Performance loss is decomposed into three parts: capacity loss from memory constraints, loss from writer choice, and the gap between the continuation procedure and the ideal decoder. Under log loss, discarded task information is exactly equivalent to conditional mutual information. The paper also derives concrete sufficient record constructions and memory-risk tradeoffs for two settings: Gaussian linear regression and nonparametric regression.","experiments":"This is a theoretical work with no large-scale empirical experiments. Core results are derived analytically: in the Gaussian linear regression setting, an exact finite-dimensional sufficient record exists, can be equivalently represented as synthetic sufficient demonstrations, and admits finite-bit perturbation bounds for posterior prediction.
